## Mission

Years ago, I once saw TJ Holowaychunk, a GitHub open-source project author and full-stack engineer, say the following:

"I don't read books, never went to school, I just read other people's code and always wonder how things work"。

Since then, I have believed that reading source code and thinking about code architecture and program logic from the perspective of experienced developers is the best way to improve programming skills.

Therefore, GitHub has naturally become my favorite developer platform.

With the original intention of exploring the value of open source, GitHubDaily was officially established on October 10, 2015.

Through this initiative, we hope to help developers discover the hottest open-source projects, stay on top of the latest technology trends, broaden their technical horizons, and improve their programming skills by learning from open-source projects.

To date, GitHubDaily has shared over 10,000 open-source projects, covering topics including but not limited to open-source technical resources, developer tools, programming websites, and production-ready applications on GitHub.

In addition to GitHub, we have also started helping developers promote and share high-quality open-source projects across multiple social media platforms, exploring their future technical application potential.

If you would like to receive the latest GitHub open-source project news, you can follow us below 👇

- WeChat Official Account: [GitHubDaily](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/GitHubDaily/GitHubDaily/master/assets/weixin.png)
- Weibo: [@GitHubDaily](https://weibo.com/GitHubDaily)
- Zhihu: [@GitHubDaily](https://www.zhihu.com/people/githubdaily)
- X (Twitter)：[@GitHubDaily](https://x.com/intent/follow?screen_name=GitHub_Daily)

> If you have a great open-source project, feel free to recommend it (or self-recommend) in this repository's [issues](https://github.com/GitHubDaily/GitHubDaily/issues/new). We look forward to your contributions.

### AI Tools

Project|Description
---|---
[ai-comic-factory](https://github.com/jbilcke-hf/ai-comic-factory)|An open source and free online AI comic generation tool that supports the generation of comic panels by selecting comic style, screen layout and inputting text prompts. It is developed based on LLM and SDXL technology. LLM and image generation models can be customized to provide Hugging Face online experience.
[AI-Couplet](https://github.com/GitHubDaily/AI-Couplet)|An AI Spring Festival couplet generation tool developed based on Claude API. You only need to enter keywords to quickly generate upper and lower couplets and horizontal batches. The interface is simple and festive. It is completely implemented using ByteDance AI programming tool Trae.
[gptme](https://github.com/ErikBjare/gptme)|A powerful open source terminal AI assistant that can execute local code, read and write files, search and browse web pages, and perform visual recognition operations on the terminal. It supports mainstream LLM providers such as OpenAI and Anthropic, and can also implement local services through llama.cpp.
[zerox](https://github.com/getomni-ai/zerox)|A simple and easy-to-use OCR document tool that can convert PDF, Docx and other files into images with one click, then identify the image information through OCR, return the Markdown format file, provide an online experience address, and support self-deployment and use.
[note-gen](https://github.com/codexu/note-gen)|A cross-end AI note-taking application focused on recording and writing. It combines recording, writing and AI to quickly organize fragmented knowledge and assist in completing complete notes. It provides a Markdown editor that supports functions such as outlines, mathematical formulas, charts, and flow charts.
[RealtimeSTT](https://github.com/KoljaB/RealtimeSTT)|A powerful and efficient speech-to-text tool that supports start and stop speaking state monitoring, specified wake word triggering, and real-time speech conversion to text. It has the characteristics of low latency, fast transcription speed, and high accuracy. It is compatible with Windows and macOS systems.
[LazyCat-Bookmark-Cleaner](https://github.com/Alanrk/LazyCat-Bookmark-Cleaner)|An AI tool focused on bookmark management. It supports one-click cleaning of invalid, duplicate bookmarks and empty folders, and provides classification statistics, access frequency analysis and organization suggestions. All functions are run locally, without uploading data to the server to ensure security and privacy.
[fullmoon-ios](https://github.com/mainframecomputer/fullmoon-ios)|An application for chatting with local large models. It can be used directly without registration. It runs locally offline and has fast response speed. Local storage of chat records ensures security and privacy. It supports custom themes, fonts and prompt words. It is currently compatible with Llama 3.2 1B and Llama 3.2 3B models.
[openai-edge-tts](https://github.com/travisvn/openai-edge-tts)|An open source free text-to-speech API that leverages Microsoft Edge's online voice service. Provides text-to-speech function, which can be used like OpenAI voice. It supports multiple audio formats such as mp3 and aac, and can freely adjust the playback speed.
[agent-service-toolkit](https://github.com/JoshuaC215/agent-service-toolkit)|A toolkit for quickly building and improving AI agent services. It integrates tools such as LangGraph Agent, FastAPI service and Streamlit application, and provides templates to help users easily build and run exclusive Agents.
[company-researcher](https://github.com/exa-labs/company-researcher)|An open source AI tool on GitHub. Enter the company website link to collect comprehensive company information from the Internet, including organizational structure, products, financing status, official media accounts, etc.
[RTranslator](https://github.com/niedev/RTranslator)|A real-time translation AI tool that can be used offline. It supports real-time translation of the other party's language into a language that the user understands to achieve simultaneous interpretation. It provides three methods of dialogue, intercom and text translation, and the model is stored locally.
[nv-ingest](https://github.com/NVIDIA/nv-ingest)|A tool for large-scale document information extraction and structured processing. It supports parsing PDF, Word, PPT and other formats. It can extract text, tables, charts and images, convert the content into structured JSON format through OCR and store it in a vector database.
[Riona-AI-Agent](https://github.com/David-patrick-chuks/Riona-AI-Agent)|A social media automation tool that can complete daily operations such as logging in, liking, forwarding, publishing posts, and replying to comments. It supports fine-tuning and training of personalized roles based on account historical data.
[Resume-Matcher](https://github.com/srbhr/Resume-Matcher)|An AI-based resume optimization tool that customizes and optimizes resumes based on job descriptions, identifies matching keywords, improves resume readability, and provides in-depth improvement functions.
[ebook2audiobookXTTS](https://github.com/DrewThomasson/ebook2audiobookXTTS)|An open source AI tool for converting e-books to audio books. It integrates Caliber, XXTSv2 and other tools to achieve one-click conversion of e-books. It supports voice cloning and 1107 languages, and can handle 20 formats such as epub, pdf, and mobi.
[FitDiT](https://github.com/BoyuanJiang/FitDiT)|A high-fidelity AI virtual try-on open source model on GitHub. It supports controlling the upper body, lower body or whole body to virtually change clothes. It can automatically generate regional masks for one-click try-on, and the details of the clothes are well preserved.
[Ollama-OCR](https://github.com/imanoop7/Ollama-OCR)|A powerful OCR tool that can be used offline, runs locally with Ollama, supports multiple visual models such as LLaVA 7B, Llama 3.2 Vision, etc., can output multiple file formats such as Markdown, plain text, JSON, etc., and supports parallel processing of multiple images.
[khoj](https://github.com/khoj-ai/khoj)|An AI tool that helps build a personal second brain. It can combine uploaded local files and Internet information to provide accurate and relevant answers. It supports local and online LLM models such as Llama 3, Qwen, and ChatGPT.
[ai-renamer](https://github.com/ozgrozer/ai-renamer)|A file batch renaming tool based on the Ollama local model, which can intelligently identify files, images or video content in a specified directory and automatically rename it. It supports Gemma, Llama and other models as well as custom parameters such as language and customization.
[amurex](https://github.com/thepersonalaicompany/amurex)|An AI tool that improves meeting efficiency. It supports real-time suggestions, meeting transcription and summary, and one-click email sending. It can be seamlessly integrated into existing workflows and can be installed through a Chrome plug-in. Currently, it is only compatible with the Google Meet platform.
[Gemini-Search](https://github.com/ammaarreshi/Gemini-Search)|An open source search engine based on Google search, which can obtain search content in real time and use Gemini to summarize it. It uses a simple interface to clearly display relevant content and sources.
[PRIME](https://github.com/PRIME-RL/PRIME)|PRIME, an AI project based on reinforcement learning technology, uses only 8 graphics cards to train a 7B model in a short time, surpassing GPT-4o and Llama-3.1 70B in mathematical reasoning capabilities. It also proposes an algorithm that can significantly improve the model's reasoning capabilities without relying on high-quality data.
[browser-use-webui](https://github.com/warmshao/browser-use-webui)|An AI agent browser automation tool developed based on Browser Use optimization. It provides a simple and easy-to-use visual WebUI operation interface to interact with the browser to complete complex automation tasks. It supports Gemini, OpenAI, DeepSeek and other large language models.
[miniperplx](https://github.com/zaidmukaddam/miniperplx)|An open source search engine based on AI that integrates Claude/Grok AI and multiple APIs to implement functions such as weather query, code running, map navigation, etc., and provides a streamlined interface to clearly display the search content.
[gitingest](https://github.com/cyclotruc/gitingest)|A tool to help you quickly understand open source projects. Enter a GitHub link to automatically summarize and generate Markdown text that is easy for LLM to read, including project summary, directory structure, README content, etc.
[GenAI_Agents](https://github.com/NirDiamant/GenAI_Agents)|A learning guide for building generative AI agents, including 40+ construction cases from basic to complex scenarios, such as simple Q&A, journey planning, contract analysis and network query summary, etc., and provides detailed documentation and implementation tutorials.
[crawl4ai](https://github.com/unclecode/crawl4ai)|A simple, easy-to-use and efficient open source web crawler tool. Just enter a web page link to crawl and output a data format suitable for training LLM. It is fast and has strong anti-crawling capabilities. It supports crawling multiple URLs and extracting media data such as images, audios, and videos.
[papermark](https://github.com/mfts/papermark)|DocSend is an open source alternative document sharing platform that provides secure document sharing, AI analysis, access tracking and data export functions. It supports customized brand display and self-deployment, and is open source without functional restrictions.
[OpenHands](https://github.com/All-Hands-AI/OpenHands)|An AI-driven multi-agent platform for software development. Multiple agents form "AI programmers", which can perform development tasks such as code modification, command running, web browsing, API calls, and copying code snippets from StackOverflow.
[open-canvas](https://github.com/langchain-ai/open-canvas)|An open source AI interactive interface that supports mainstream LLM and local models. It has a built-in memory function to remember usage habits and styles. It is compatible with Claude, Gemini, ChatGPT and Ollama models.
[harbor](https://github.com/av/harbor)|A containerized LLM toolbox that can install and run various LLM backends, APIs, frontends and related services through simple commands. It integrates Open WebUI, ComfyUI, Ollama, Dify and other tools, and supports automatic downloading of models and custom configurations.
[VideoFusion](https://github.com/271374667/VideoFusion)|A one-stop video batch processing tool for inexperienced video creators. It realizes video preprocessing without having to worry about complex parameters. It has the functions of automatic black border removal, intelligent splicing, frame filling, resolution adjustment, white balance, AI audio noise reduction and batch processing of multiple videos.
[prompt-optimizer](https://github.com/linshenkx/prompt-optimizer)|A tool for optimizing and testing AI prompt words. It supports the use of web applications and Chrome plug-ins. It can optimize prompt words with one click and perform multiple rounds of iterative improvements to improve the accuracy of AI responses.
[ai-trend-publish](https://github.com/OpenAISpace/ai-trend-publish)|An AI-based trend discovery and content publishing system that supports multi-source data collection (Twitter/X, website content) and AI intelligent processing. It can automatically publish content to WeChat public accounts, and is suitable for operators of automated content creation.
[aibrix](https://github.com/vllm-project/aibrix)|An enterprise-level cloud-native GenAI inference infrastructure construction tool that provides a complete solution for large-scale language model inference deployment, management and expansion, with high-density LoRA management system and LLM gateway and routing functions.
[EPLB](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/EPLB)|A tool focused on solving the load balancing problem of MoE model training. It achieves load balancing by copying high-load experts and heuristically packaging them to different GPUs. Based on the redundant expert strategy, it combines group restriction expert routing technology to reduce data transmission between nodes. It provides two load balancing strategies, hierarchical and global, to adapt to different scenarios.
[DualPipe](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DualPipe)|A bidirectional pipeline parallel algorithm for large-scale AI model training, which solves the core efficiency problem of training and completely overlaps the forward and backward calculation-communication stages through innovative scheduling strategies while reducing pipeline bubbles.
[prompt-kit](https://github.com/ibelick/prompt-kit)|A high-quality UI component library specially designed for AI applications. It can quickly build applications such as chat experiences, AI agents, and autonomous assistants. It provides beautiful and customizable interface components, including customizable AI prompt input components and chat message display components.
[Awesome-Ollama-Server](https://github.com/forrany/Awesome-Ollama-Server)|A system for monitoring and detecting Ollama services. It supports batch detection services, real-time display of status and results, automatic FOFA scanning function, and provides a modern web visual interface and multi-language support in Chinese and English.
[claude-code](https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code)|Claude Code, an AI programming assistant running in the terminal, supports understanding and operating local code libraries. It can perform basic coding tasks, explain code logic, handle Git workflow, answer architectural questions, perform repair testing and code formatting.
[R1-Onevision](https://github.com/Fancy-MLLM/R1-Onevision)|An open source multi-modal large language model capable of deep reasoning, especially good at visual reasoning tasks, and performs well in many fields such as mathematics, science, image understanding and logical reasoning, surpassing Qwen2 in multiple reasoning benchmark tests.
[SurveyX](https://github.com/IAAR-Shanghai/SurveyX)|A GitHub open source academic tool based on a large language model, which can generate high-quality academic papers and surveys based on paper titles or keywords, and provides 30+ generated paper examples covering computer vision, medical imaging and other fields.
[deepchat](https://github.com/ThinkInAIXYZ/deepchat)|A simple and easy-to-use third-party open source client for DeepSeek, it supports multiple model cloud service providers such as DeepSeek, Silicon Flow, and Ollama, as well as multi-channel chat concurrency, and has complete Markdown rendering and code highlighting functions.
[apple-mcp](https://github.com/Dhravya/apple-mcp)|An Apple native tool set suitable for the MCP protocol. By adding a small amount of code to the Claude desktop configuration, you can grant access to Apple's native tools. It currently supports functions such as contacts, messages, and notes, and will expand calendar, reminder implementation and other tools in the future.
[llm-scraper](https://github.com/mishushakov/llm-scraper)|A web page data extraction tool based on LLMs, which can extract structured data from any web page. It supports LLM providers such as Ollama and OpenAI and multiple input formats such as html, markdown, text, image, etc., and supports streaming objects.
[DeepSeek](https://github.com/DargonLee/DeepSeek)|A GitHub open source third-party DeepSeek iOS client. Integrate DeepSeek model capabilities, support real-time dialogue, multi-round interaction, historical record management and custom prompt words, and also have multi-topic, local message storage, real-time input and message copy functions.
[AstrBot](https://github.com/Soulter/AstrBot)|A loosely coupled multi-platform LLM chat robot and development framework that supports the access of multiple large language models such as OpenAI and Gemini. It has asynchronous processing capabilities, multi-message platform deployment support and an easy-to-use plug-in system.
[aigcpanel](https://github.com/modstart-lib/aigcpanel)|A simple and easy-to-use one-stop AI digital human system that supports video digital human synthesis, speech synthesis, and cloning. It can match video images and sounds with mouth shapes, simplify local model management, and support one-click import and use of AI models.
[DeepSeek-RAG-Chatbot](https://github.com/SaiAkhil066/DeepSeek-RAG-Chatbot)|An open source and free offline advanced RAG conversation tool that can quickly and accurately retrieve information from PDF, DOCX and TXT files. It integrates DeepSeek, GraphRAG and other technologies, and has chat history memory and improved error handling mechanism.
[DeepEP](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepEP)|An expert parallel communication library for hybrid expert model training and inference, providing efficient and optimized all-to-all communication capabilities, while supporting intra-node NVLink and inter-node RDMA communication, with high-throughput training and inference pre-filled kernels and low-latency inference kernels.
[anx-reader](https://github.com/Anxcye/anx-reader)|An open source and free e-book reader that supports mainstream formats such as EPUB and MOBI. It has a modern interface design and multiple AI capabilities. It can synchronize reading progress across devices through WebDAV, providing an intelligent and focused pure reading experience.
[mastra](https://github.com/mastra-ai/mastra)|A TypeScript development framework for quickly building AI applications. It integrates common tools such as Agents, Tools, Workflow, RAG and evaluation, and can be used directly to improve development efficiency.
[arxiv-mcp-server](https://github.com/blazickjp/arxiv-mcp-server)|An MCP server for easy access and search of arXiv papers. It supports filtering and searching papers by date range and category, and can download and read paper contents, providing AI assistants with fast retrieval and complete access capabilities.
[paper_to_podcast](https://github.com/Azzedde/paper_to_podcast)|An open source tool that can convert academic papers into AI podcasts. Just provide a PDF academic paper and it can be converted into a three-person dialogue podcast. It is simple to deploy and requires configuring an OpenAI API key and running a Python script.
[vibe-draw](https://github.com/martin226/vibe-draw)|A simple and easy-to-use 3D modeling tool that supports free drawing of 2D sketches and one-click conversion to 3D models. It has the technical features of AI-assisted optimization of sketches to improve details and quality.
[ruoyi-ai](https://github.com/ageerle/ruoyi-ai)|An open source solution built on a one-stop AI assistant platform, including a complete system such as front-end applications, back-end management and small programs. It can be used out of the box and is open source based on the MIT license and can be freely used for commercial use.
[ClaudeComputerCommander](https://github.com/wonderwhy-er/ClaudeComputerCommander)|An MCP server that enables Claude desktop applications to execute terminal commands and edit files. Through the model context protocol, Claude can execute terminal commands, manage processes, provide file editing and search and replacement functions, and can be used with one click through Smithery and npx.
[star-vector](https://github.com/joanrod/star-vector)|A multi-modal visual language model focusing on SVG image generation. It supports the generation of SVG code from text or images. It performs well in generating icons, logos, and charts. It provides two versions of 1B and 8B parameters and is published on HuggingFace.
[pdf-craft](https://github.com/oomol-lab/pdf-craft)|An open source PDF conversion tool focused on scanned book processing. It can extract text, filter headers and footers, and handle cross-page connections. It uses a local AI model to convert PDF to Markdown or structured EPUB e-books without the need for an Internet connection.
[ClaraVerse](https://github.com/badboysm890/ClaraVerse)|A privacy-first open source tool for building AI assistants and agents that can use open source models to conduct AI chats, create agents, and convert them into fully functional applications on local devices. All data is processed locally without leaving the device to achieve privacy protection.
[gurubase](https://github.com/Gurubase/gurubase)|An AI Q&A assistant that can be created based on any content. It supports adding web pages, PDFs, videos and GitHub warehouses as knowledge sources. It can build professional Q&A assistants and embed them into websites or integrate with Slack and Discord. It supports real-time updating of data sources.
[docling](https://github.com/docling-project/docling)|An open source framework for document processing that supports parsing of multiple formats such as PDF, DOCX, XLSX, HTML, and images. It can be seamlessly integrated with mainstream generative AI frameworks and supports local execution to protect sensitive data.
[XianyuAutoAgent](https://github.com/shaxiu/XianyuAutoAgent)|An open source tool for Xianyu store operations that implements an AI intelligent customer service system with 24-hour automated on-call duty, supporting context-aware dialogue, multi-expert collaborative decision-making, and intelligent bargaining functions.
[YT-Navigator](https://github.com/wassim249/YT-Navigator)|An open source tool for efficiently searching and browsing YouTube channel content. It searches for video-specific information through chat conversations and jumps to relevant time points. It supports semantic search of channel content to quickly locate relevant video clips.
[MagicQuill](https://github.com/magic-quill/MagicQuill)|An interactive image editing AI tool on GitHub, it can realize editing tasks such as adding elements, removing objects, and adjusting screen colors through simple strokes and prompt words. It provides canvas tools such as dragging, rotating, and adjusting brush sizes, and supports the selection of different painting models.
[my-yt](https://github.com/christian-fei/my-yt)|A YouTube third-party web client developed based on yt-dlp. The interface is clean and concise with no ads. It supports channel subscription, video downloading and offline playback, and automatic removal of sponsored content. It can integrate local AI models to summarize videos and deploy them through Node.js or Docker.
[mlx-lm](https://github.com/ml-explore/mlx-lm)|A large model processing Python toolkit for Apple chips that supports text generation and model fine-tuning. It is integrated with Hugging Face Hub and can call thousands of large language models with a single command.
[easy-dataset](https://github.com/ConardLi/easy-dataset)|A large model fine-tuning data set creation tool that provides an intuitive interface to support file upload and intelligent content segmentation. It can automatically generate questions and high-quality training data. It supports the export of Alpaca, JSON and other formats and is compatible with OpenAI formats.
[ReasonGraph](https://github.com/ZongqianLi/ReasonGraph)|A large model reasoning process visualization tool that can visually display and analyze the execution process of multiple reasoning methods, making model reasoning transparent and interpretable.
[markpdfdown](https://github.com/jorben/markpdfdown)|A PDF to Markdown tool based on a multi-modal large language model that supports PDF document format conversion and fully retains complex layout elements such as tables and formulas. It uses AI technology to understand the document structure and supports custom model parameter configuration and specified page range conversion.
[tools](https://github.com/simonw/tools)|A front-end tool set built by foreign developer Simon with the help of AI, including a variety of practical HTML+JavaScript gadgets. The developer recorded in detail the process of quickly building tools using LLM.
[SoftWhisper](https://github.com/NullMagic2/SoftWhisper)|An efficient and convenient audio-to-text tool, based on Whisper.cpp, it has very few dependencies and supports CPU+GPU acceleration, greatly improving the transcription speed. It is dozens of times faster than OpenAI's Whisper. It only takes 2-3 minutes to complete the transcription of 2 hours of audio.
[Coze-on-Wechat](https://github.com/JC0v0/Coze-on-Wechat)|An open source tool that quickly connects the Coze platform AI robot to WeChat. It supports text chat, voice interaction and plug-in functions, and provides a Web backend management interface to configure and manage the robot.
[MaxKB](https://github.com/1Panel-dev/MaxKB)|An open source AI knowledge base Q&A system that supports uploading PDFs, Word documents or entering URLs with one click to crawl and automatically process and generate Q&A capabilities. It can accurately understand questions and give complete answers. It can be connected to DeepSeek R1, Llama 3, OpenAI and other large models to meet privacy and cost requirements.
[Sidekick](https://github.com/johnbean393/Sidekick)|A local AI assistant for Mac that supports getting information from files, folders and websites to chat with local LLM. All conversations run in an offline environment to ensure data security.
[local-deep-research](https://github.com/LearningCircuit/local-deep-research)|An AI research assistant that can be run locally, can perform in-depth and iterative research analysis, supports a variety of LLM and network searches, and not only protects privacy but also provides powerful research capabilities.
[mcp-playwright](https://github.com/executeautomation/mcp-playwright)|A tool that provides browser automation capabilities for large models, enabling intelligent AI web browsing and operations. Through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, LLM can interact with web pages, take screenshots, and execute JavaScript in a real browser environment.
[Open-Sora](https://github.com/hpcaitech/Open-Sora)|An open source SOTA video generation large model that can generate 720p high-resolution and 24 FPS smooth videos. It uses a 3D full attention mechanism and MMDiT architecture to reduce the cost by 5-10 times through efficient training methods. The inference time is reduced to 3 minutes on a single card. The actions are controllable and the scene switching effect is rich.
[blender-mcp](https://github.com/ahujasid/blender-mcp)|An AI-assisted 3D content creation tool based on MCP, which allows Claude to directly control Blender to automatically perform 3D modeling, scene creation and model operations, realizing two-way communication between Claude AI and Blender.
[agents](https://github.com/cloudflare/agents)|A development framework for building intelligent stateful AI agents that supports state management and persistent memory, allowing AI agents to persist, think, and evolve at the edge of the network.
[hipixel](https://github.com/okooo5km/hipixel)|An AI image super-resolution tool focusing on workflow efficiency and macOS native experience. Provides high-quality AI image enlargement function, supports multiple image formats, and uses SwiftUI interface to create native macOS applications.
[rlama](https://github.com/dontizi/rlama)|A local document intelligent question and answer tool that can seamlessly integrate local Ollama model creation, management and use of the RAG system. It supports text, code and various office document formats to realize intelligent question and answer on documents.
[story-flicks](https://github.com/alecm20/story-flicks)|A story scene video generation tool based on AI technology that automatically generates a complete video based on the input story theme, including story content, pictures, audio and subtitles, and supports multiple text models.
[mcp-server-chatsum](https://github.com/chatmcp/mcp-server-chatsum)|A WeChat group chat message summary tool that combines the MCP protocol. It collects and stores WeChat messages in real time through wechaty, supports querying and summarizing group chat history, and uses the Claude desktop version as the interactive entrance.
[botgroup.chat](https://github.com/maojindao55/botgroup.chat)|An AI chat application built based on React and Cloudflare Pages, it supports multiple AI characters to participate in conversations at the same time, provides an interactive experience similar to group chat, and can easily create multi-character conversation scenes.
[nanobrowser](https://github.com/nanobrowser/nanobrowser)|An AI web page automation tool that can be used as an open source alternative to OpenAI Operator. It uses multi-agent system collaboration to complete complex web page operations. It supports running locally in the browser and provides flexible LLM options.
[autoMate](https://github.com/yuruotong1/autoMate)|An AI-driven local automation open source tool that uses natural language to describe tasks to realize automatic operation of the computer interface and complete complex workflows without programming knowledge. It integrates AI and RPA technology.
[fastrtc](https://github.com/freddyaboulton/fastrtc)|A Python library for real-time communication with built-in speech recognition and automatic dialogue management functions. [ai-hedge-fund](https://github.com/virattt/ai-hedge-fund)|An AI-driven hedge fund proof-of-concept project for educational purposes, simulating the investment decision-making process through 11 AI agents with different roles working together, including well-known investors, technical analysts, fundamental analysis and other agents.
[klee-client](https://github.com/signerlabs/klee-client)|A secure and localized AI desktop application with built-in RAG knowledge base and Markdown note support, which can run open source large models locally, enabling complete offline use and zero data collection to protect privacy and security.
[ComfyUI-RMBG](https://github.com/1038lab/ComfyUI-RMBG)|A background removal plug-in based on ComfyUI that supports accurate segmentation of objects, faces, clothing, and fashion elements. [storm](https://github.com/stanford-oval/storm)|An innovative AI writing tool open sourced from Stanford University. [chat-with-audios](https://github.com/patchy631/ai-engineering-hub/tree/main/chat-with-audios)|A RAG application project built based on audio files, which can quickly convert audio files into text and support AI question and answer. [composio](https://github.com/composiohq/composio)|A toolkit for quickly building AI agents. [paperless-ai](https://github.com/clusterzx/paperless-ai)|A tool that provides AI support for the Paperless-ngx document management system. [ollama-for-amd](https://github.com/likelovewant/ollama-for-amd)|An open source project based on the official Ollama modification, it adds support for more AMD graphics cards, quickly starts and runs large models such as DeepSeek, Llama 3, and Gemma, and is compatible with gfx803, gfx908, gfx90c, gfx1011, gfx1032, gfx1100 and other models.
[writing-helper](https://github.com/GeekyWizKid/writing-helper)|An AI writing assistant based on GitHub open source. [yogu-chat-app](https://github.com/xiaozi/yogu-chat-app)|A simple and feature-rich AI chat desktop application that supports web search, chat history management, multi-language, file upload and multi-theme switching. [viral-predictor](https://github.com/Azure-Vision/viral-predictor)|A popular social platform post prediction tool for self-media creators. [MTranServer](https://github.com/xxnuo/MTranServer)|An ultra-low resource consumption offline translation server that supports translation of major languages ​​around the world and has a quality comparable to Google Translate. [shandu](https://github.com/jolovicdev/shandu)|A Deep Research open source replacement tool based on LLM and web crawler technology, it can conduct comprehensive research on any topic and automatically generate a comprehensive report containing complete references. [instructor](https://github.com/instructor-ai/instructor)|A Python library that helps developers obtain structured data from large language models. [code2prompt](https://github.com/mufeedvh/code2prompt)|An open source tool for code base conversion that can quickly traverse the project directory to build a tree structure and collect file information, automatically generate formatted Markdown prompt word documents, and support customizing prompt content through Handlebars templates.
[gitpodcast](https://github.com/BandarLabs/gitpodcast)|An open source tool that can convert GitHub projects into podcasts. [AingDesk](https://github.com/aingdesk/AingDesk)|A local AI model deployment tool that can deploy hundreds of AI models such as DeepSeek to a local computer with one click. [langgraph-swarm](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph-swarm)|A Python library used to realize the collaborative work of multiple AI agents, allowing multiple agents to automatically take over conversations based on their own expertise, achieving dynamic switching and seamless connection between agents, and supporting short-term and long-term memory functions to ensure conversation coherence.
[WhisperChain](https://github.com/chrischoy/WhisperChain)|A practical tool on GitHub that converts speech into text in real time and automatically polishes the quality of the transcribed text. It can copy the converted text to the clipboard, which helps save typing time and improve work efficiency.
[probly](https://github.com/PragmaticMachineLearning/probly)|An intelligent spreadsheet tool for data analysis. It can complete the entire data processing process in one stop, automatically analyze data and generate charts through AI, and its functions are more powerful than Excel.

[manga-image-translator](https://github.com/zyddnys/manga-image-translator)|A translation tool for comic images that supports one-click translation into Japanese, Chinese, English and Korean. [mcp-send-email](https://github.com/ykhli/mcp-send-email)|A model context protocol (MCP) case based on Resend API, which implements the one-click sending function after Cursor or Claude composes an email. No need to copy and paste the content. It is suitable for MCP learning.

[claude-task-master](https://github.com/eyaltoledano/claude-task-master)|A tool that assists developers in using AI for project development. [WatermarkRemover-AI](https://github.com/D-Ogi/WatermarkRemover-AI)|An open source and free AI watermark removal tool that supports single and folder batch processing of image watermarks. [magentic-ui](https://github.com/microsoft/magentic-ui)|An open source human-machine collaboration web page automation tool from Microsoft that supports collaboration with AI assistants to plan and edit complex web page tasks. [Ghost-Downloader-3](https://github.com/XiaoYouChR/Ghost-Downloader-3)|A cross-platform file download tool that supports AI intelligent acceleration and multi-threaded concurrent downloads to improve speed and stability. It has intelligent block downloading without merging files, scheduled task management and browser plug-in optimization functions. It is compatible with Linux, Windows and macOS systems.
[mcp-prompt-server](https://github.com/joeseesun/mcp-prompt-server)|An MCP server used to manage and call Prompt. Register commonly used Prompt templates as tools that can be called by parameterization, support natural language calling and combination of tools to implement complex workflows, have hot loading functions and YAML expansion capabilities, and are suitable for mainstream editors such as Raycast and Cursor.
[codeflash](https://github.com/codeflash-ai/codeflash)|An open source tool focused on Python code optimization, which can optimize the entire code base and improve performance with one click. Generate multiple optimization solutions through large models, automatically test correctness and performance benchmarks, and create pull requests with the best optimizations for review.
[nanoDeepResearch](https://github.com/liyuan24/nanoDeepResearch)|An open source project for a deep understanding of the underlying workings of OpenAI Deep Research. Implement a complete ReAct agent system and multi-expert collaboration mechanism, transparently display the decision-making process and generated content, support network search, web crawling and Python programming tools, and build in-depth research Agents from scratch without relying on existing frameworks.
[news-agents](https://github.com/eugeneyan/news-agents)|An open source news information aggregation tool for terminal developers. Automatically capture the latest technology and business information from multiple sources such as Hacker News, implement multi-agent parallel processing through Amazon Q, MCP, and tmux, and generate separate abstracts and complete summaries of sources. Information can be obtained intuitively and efficiently without switching windows.
[awesome-cursor-rules-mdc](https://github.com/sanjeed5/awesome-cursor-rules-mdc)|An MDC rule file generation tool for Cursor that can automatically collect best practices from each library to generate structured content. It uses Exa semantic search and LLM technology to support parallel processing and progress tracking to simplify the rule creation process.
[stagewise](https://github.com/stagewise-io/stagewise)|An AI development assistant tool for front-end developers, used to simplify the UI style modification process. You can select elements and add comments directly in the browser, automatically integrate screenshots, DOM structure and metadata and send them to the AI ​​assistant. It supports mainstream front-end frameworks and only takes effect in development mode. No additional configuration is required to connect to the VS Code extension.
[MathModelAgent](https://github.com/jihe520/MathModelAgent)|An AI assistant that can automatically complete the entire process of mathematical modeling. It has built-in agents such as coder, paper hand, and modeling hand, which can fully automatically analyze problems, model, write code, correct errors, and generate complete papers in a format. The coder has a reflection module and a local code interpreter.
[mcp-server-chart](https://github.com/antvis/mcp-server-chart)|An MCP server that provides professional-level data visualization capabilities for AI assistants. It can directly generate 15+ high-quality charts such as bar charts, line charts, and pie charts in AI conversations. It uses the AntV visual chart generation framework.
[arxiv_summarizer](https://github.com/Shaier/arxiv_summarizer)|An open source tool that improves the efficiency of paper reading. It supports single and batch paper summaries and keyword searches. It uses the free Gemini API to automatically extract and summarize the content of arXiv papers. It can also be set to automatically track the latest papers every day and generate abstracts for storage.
[papersgpt-for-zotero](https://github.com/papersgpt/papersgpt-for-zotero)|A Zotero literature tool plug-in that integrates AI models to enable dialogue with papers on the Zotero interface. It supports local open source models and mainstream API models. It can analyze multiple documents, generate reviews, ensure data privacy, and seamlessly integrate Zotero.
[graphiti](https://github.com/getzep/graphiti)|An open source framework for time-aware knowledge graphs built for AI agents in dynamic environments. It builds a queryable graph by continuously integrating multi-source data to achieve incremental updates, efficient retrieval and accurate historical queries. It supports bitemporal data models, hybrid retrieval and MCP server integration.
[auto-dev](https://github.com/unit-mesh/auto-dev)|An AI development auxiliary plug-in for IntelliJ IDEA, it integrates a multi-functional smart workspace, provides AI code generation, testing and document creation, supports multi-language development and the entire SDLC process, has a built-in optimized programming model and allows customization of LLM servers and team-level prompt words.
[moodiary](https://github.com/ZhuJHua/moodiary)|A comprehensive cross-platform diary application that supports various recording forms such as text, pictures, audio, and paintings. It is equipped with local AI analysis functions to ensure that private data will not be uploaded to third-party servers. It covers Android, iOS, Windows, and Mac platforms.
[terminator](https://github.com/mediar-ai/terminator)|An innovative open source automated AI tool that directly analyzes computer applications to achieve fast and accurate control. It is much faster than traditional methods, supports occlusion or background window operations, and does not fail due to interface changes. Currently, it mainly supports Windows systems, and macOS only supports some functions.
[speakr](https://github.com/murtaza-nasir/speakr)|An AI conference assistant that ensures data privacy and security. It supports offline operation, can transcribe meeting recordings into text and generate summaries, and can also conduct interactive Q&A with the meeting content through chat, and all data is stored locally.
[docext](https://github.com/NanoNets/docext)|A localized open source document information extraction tool that does not require OCR. It can flexibly extract custom fields and table information from various documents. It supports the use of pre-built templates and uses visual language models to achieve accurate identification and ensure data security and privacy.
[ACE-Step](https://github.com/ace-step/ACE-Step)|An open source music model comparable to Suno, it can generate high-quality music in a very short time and is 15 times faster than traditional AI models. By combining the diffusion generation model and lightweight Transformer, REPA technology is used to achieve semantic alignment during training, so that the generated music can maintain a high degree of coherence and accurately align lyrics.
[void](https://github.com/voideditor/void)|An open source replacement programming tool for Cursor. It has powerful programming functions similar to Cursor. It supports intelligent code completion by pressing the Tab key. It can directly connect to any AI model or use a local deployment model without going through a third-party server.
[flowgram.ai](https://github.com/bytedance/flowgram.ai)|A visual workflow construction engine based on node editing, providing two modes of fixed layout and free connection layout and complete interactive practice, focusing on empowering AI capabilities for workflow, and supporting node drag and drop operations.
[buster](https://github.com/buster-so/buster)|An AI data analyst tool for data operation scenarios. It supports natural language query data and generates visual charts and dynamic dashboards. It manages models and dashboards through coding, supports CI/CD, version control and seamless integration with dbt tools, and provides AI intelligent suggestions to improve data models and improve data quality.
[SurfSense](https://github.com/MODSetter/SurfSense)|An open source intelligent knowledge base platform integrating the core functions of NotebookLM and Perplexity. It supports uploading 27 file formats to build a private knowledge base, obtains reference answers through natural language interaction, can connect to external resources such as search engines and quickly generate podcast content, uses advanced RAG technology to improve retrieval accuracy, and provides cross-browser extensions to save web pages and private deployment options.
[arxiv_daily_aigc](https://github.com/onion-liu/arxiv_daily_aigc)|An open source project for screening the latest AIGC papers on arXiv. [OpenDeepWiki](https://github.com/AIDotNet/OpenDeepWiki)|An open source tool that helps developers quickly understand the code structure. It generates a complete knowledge base and Mermaid diagrams to display structural dependencies through AI in-depth analysis of project codes. It supports conversational interactive questioning of code details, custom AI model expansion, and generation of SEO-friendly documents.
[kubectl-ai](https://github.com/GoogleCloudPlatform/kubectl-ai)|A kubectl plug-in tool for Kubernetes cluster management. Allow users to directly control the cluster through natural language without having to memorize complex commands. It supports various AI models such as Gemini and OpenAI as well as local offline models, has interactive session capabilities, and can be used with other command line tools.
[cs-paper-checklist](https://github.com/yzhao062/cs-paper-checklist)|A quality inspection checklist for computer science papers, mainly used to check the writing quality, structural rationality and content presentation of conference and journal submissions. [agent-squad](https://github.com/awslabs/agent-squad)|A lightweight and flexible open source multi-AI agent coordination framework that can intelligently route to the most appropriate AI agent based on the sent content, and maintain dialogue context between different agents. [excel-mcp-server](https://github.com/haris-musa/excel-mcp-server)|A practical MCP server that directly operates Excel files through AI assistant to create, read, modify table data, apply formats, create charts and pivot tables and other professional Excel functions without installing Microsoft Excel office software.
[MCP-SuperAssistant](https://github.com/srbhptl39/MCP-SuperAssistant)|A browser plug-in for AI dialogue web pages, it unlocks MCP tool calls on the ChatGPT, Gemini, and Grok interfaces. [AI-Media2Doc](https://github.com/hanshuaikang/AI-Media2Doc)|A self-media content creation tool that can convert videos and audios into documents in various styles such as knowledge notes and content summaries with one click. It also supports AI dialogue questions based on video content.
[cloi](https://github.com/cloi-ai/cloi)|A terminal debugging AI tool that runs completely locally. It has a built-in Phi-4 model and supports switching. It can analyze errors, automatically generate repair plans and directly apply modifications after approval, improving debugging efficiency. It is completely open source and free.
[deep-research](https://github.com/u14app/deep-research)|An open source replacement tool that focuses on quickly generating in-depth research reports. [zotero-pdf2zh](https://github.com/guaguastandup/zotero-pdf2zh)|A PDF translation plug-in for Zotero, which can translate English papers with one click and retain the original text comparison. [company-research-agent](https://github.com/pogjester/company-research-agent)|An open source project for automatically generating company research reports. [kotaemon](https://github.com/Cinnamon/kotaemon)|An open source project for document interaction that can extract information through chat. [notebooks](https://github.com/unslothai/notebooks)|A one-stop collection of fine-tuning notes for large models, providing 40+ notebooks covering mainstream open source models such as Llama 3, Phi 4, Mistral, Qwen 2.5, etc. Each notebook contains detailed notes and guidelines to facilitate direct fine-tuning of the training model.
[transformerlab-app](https://github.com/transformerlab/transformerlab-app)|A full-process tool for large language model development. [deepwiki-open](https://github.com/AsyncFuncAI/deepwiki-open)|An open source tool for efficiently understanding code bases. [interview-coder-withoupaywall-opensource](https://github.com/greeneu/interview-coder-withoupaywall-opensource)|An auxiliary open source tool for technical interviewers. [tolgee-platform](https://github.com/tolgee/tolgee-platform)|An open source tool designed for internationalization of front-end web applications. [superdesign](https://github.com/superdesigndev/superdesign)|An open source AI design agent directly integrated into the IDE, it generates a complete UI interface, reusable components and wireframes through natural language prompts, and supports mainstream AI editors such as Cursor, Windsurf, and VS Code.
[fireplexity](https://github.com/mendableai/fireplexity)|An ultra-fast AI search engine that can obtain real-time and reliable information faster. [MultiAgentPPT](https://github.com/johnson7788/MultiAgentPPT)|An automated PPT generation tool that can generate high-quality content by simply entering a topic and support online editing and modification. [NativeMindExtension](https://github.com/NativeMindBrowser/NativeMindExtension)|A fully localized AI assistant browser plug-in that supports intelligent dialogue, multi-tab context understanding, web page content analysis, and calls local models through Ollama and WebLLM. [knowledge-graph-llms](https://github.com/thu-vu92/knowledge-graph-llms)|An open source tool that automatically generates knowledge graphs from text. [illa-helper](https://github.com/xiao-zaiyi/illa-helper)|A browser language learning plug-in based on the "i+1" theory, which can intelligently replace some vocabulary words with the target language during web browsing and is equipped with a pronunciation learning system. [gemini-cli](https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli)|A terminal programming AI assistant that benchmarks Claude Code, based on the Gemini 2.5 Pro model, supports 1 million Token context windows, multi-modal generation applications, automated code merging, and integrates MCP protocol extensions and Google search functions.
[Twocast](https://github.com/panyanyany/Twocast)|Twocast, a GitHub open source AI blog generation tool, supports multiple input methods such as topics, web links, and documents to generate 3-5 minute two-person podcast content including audio, outlines, and scripts in a few minutes, and the generation effect is close to real people.
[enableAppleAI](https://github.com/kanshurichard/enableAppleAI)|An open source tool for domestic Mac devices that can permanently turn on the Apple Intelligence function. [openai-cs-agents-demo](https://github.com/openai/openai-cs-agents-demo)|An airline intelligent customer service system built based on OpenAI Agents SDK, which can automatically identify the type of problem and route it to the corresponding professional Agent. [claudia](https://github.com/getAsterisk/claudia)|A GUI interface tool built for Claude Code. [Claude-Code-Usage-Monitor](https://github.com/Maciek-roboblog/Claude-Code-Usage-Monitor)|An open source tool for monitoring Claude Code token usage. [tools](https://github.com/strands-agents/tools)|An AI Agents development framework that provides more than 30 practical tools covering core functions such as file operations and system commands. [yap](https://github.com/finnvoor/yap)|A local speech transcription tool designed specifically for macOS. It can process audio and video files and support multi-language transcription. It can be used with yt-dlp to download YouTube videos for direct transcription. It runs completely offline to ensure data security and privacy.
[BallonsTranslator](https://github.com/dmMaze/BallonsTranslator)|A comic translation tool based on deep learning that can automatically detect comic text areas and identify the content to complete translation, while maintaining the original color, outline, angle and other layout effects. [obs-backgroundremoval](https://github.com/locaal-ai/obs-backgroundremoval)|An OBS live broadcast background processing plug-in based on neural network. [chatlog](https://github.com/sjzar/chatlog)|An open source tool for WeChat chat record management that supports WeChat version 3.x/4.0 to automatically obtain chat records. [graph-rag-agent](https://github.com/1517005260/graph-rag-agent)|An intelligent question and answer system framework that combines GraphRAG and DeepSearch, which can answer questions and completely display the thinking trajectory and evidence sources, reproduce the core functions of GraphRAG from scratch, and improve the visibility and credibility of the AI ​​reasoning process.
[TradingAgents](https://github.com/TauricResearch/TradingAgents)|An open source project for financial transaction collaboration based on a multi-agent system. [mcp-sequential-thinking](https://github.com/arben-adm/mcp-sequential-thinking)|A thought process management server based on MCP protocol. [jaaz](https://github.com/11cafe/jaaz)|An AI design assistant that can be run locally, it realizes image editing, batch generation and unlimited canvas creation through a conversational interface. [ssh-ai-chat](https://github.com/ccbikai/ssh-ai-chat)|An AI dialogue tool based on the SSH protocol that supports direct connection to mainstream models such as DeepSeek-V3 and Gemini through the command line. [Office-PowerPoint-MCP-Server](https://github.com/GongRzhe/Office-PowerPoint-MCP-Server)|A comprehensive Office-PowerPoint-MCP server that supports the direct creation and editing of PPT through AI assistant. [agent-rules](https://github.com/steipete/agent-rules)|A collection of best practice rule configurations specially collected for AI programming assistants. [pagemagic](https://github.com/khaledh/pagemagic)|An open source browser plug-in for modifying the appearance of web pages, allowing users to describe the desired change effects through natural language, and AI will automatically generate the corresponding style code and apply it to the page in real time.
[wenyan-mcp](https://github.com/caol64/wenyan-mcp)|An MCP server for public account article processing. [mcp-chrome](https://github.com/hangwin/mcp-chrome)|An MCP server based on Chrome plug-in, which implements complex browser automation, content analysis and semantic search, and can maintain the user's login status and browser settings.
[davia](https://github.com/davialabs/davia)|An open source LLM application generation tool for developers that can quickly create interactive web applications based on shadcn/ui components. [opik](https://github.com/comet-ml/opik)|An open source evaluation platform for building, evaluating, and optimizing LLM systems. [avante.nvim](https://github.com/yetone/avante.nvim)|An open source plug-in for the Neovim editor that provides AI-driven code analysis and suggestions directly in the editor, supports intelligent dialogue with the current file or selected code base, and can apply AI suggestions with one click.
[PreenCut](https://github.com/roothch/PreenCut)|An open source video editing tool based on AI technology, it uses WhisperX to realize automatic speech recognition to generate accurate transcriptions, supports semantic search functions, and can quickly locate video clips using natural language.
[AI-worker](https://github.com/qyjoy/AI-worker)|A Cloudflare Worker tool for accessing restricted AI services. It achieves access to Gemini, Imagen and other platforms by forwarding API requests. It has a built-in simple HTML interface for easy use.
[MonkeyOCR](https://github.com/Yuliang-Liu/MonkeyOCR)|An OCR tool focused on complex document parsing. [vibetest-use](https://github.com/browser-use/vibetest-use)|An MCP server for website automation testing. It can launch multiple Agents with one click to conduct comprehensive testing of the website. [OpenAiTx](https://github.com/OpenAiTx/OpenAiTx)|An open source free tool for generating multi-language READMEs for GitHub projects. It can translate projects into 20 languages ​​with one click. [AI-Screenshot-Translator](https://github.com/Diraw/AI-Screenshot-Translator)|A screenshot translation tool that solves the problem of image scanning and foreign PDF text copying. [langchain4j](https://github.com/langchain4j/langchain4j)|An AI function integration framework designed for Java developers. [vosk-api](https://github.com/alphacep/vosk-api)|An offline open source speech recognition toolkit. [AirPosture](https://github.com/allenv0/AirPosture)|A macOS sitting posture monitoring application based on AirPods sensors, which captures and visualizes bad postures in real time, and displays information such as bad posture time and head direction to facilitate timely adjustments.
[agentic-doc](https://github.com/landing-ai/agentic-doc)|An open source Python library designed to efficiently extract structured data from complex documents. [daily-arXiv-ai-enhanced](https://github.com/dw-dengwei/daily-arXiv-ai-enhanced)|An open source tool that tracks the latest papers on arXiv every day. [dedoc](https://github.com/ispras/dedoc)|A document format conversion and structured processing tool that can automatically convert multiple format documents such as Word, PDF, and scanned documents into a unified structured format. [Perth](https://github.com/resemble-ai/Perth)|An open source audio implicit watermarking tool based on neural network technology. [onlook](https://github.com/onlook-dev/onlook)|An open source AI editor specially built for designers. It provides a visual editing interface similar to Figma. [ai-knowledge-graph](https://github.com/robert-mcdermott/ai-knowledge-graph)|An open source knowledge graph generation tool based on LLM technology that can automatically convert documents into visual knowledge graphs. [memvid](https://github.com/olow304/memvid)|An open source project that subverts the traditional vector database processing method. [chatterbox](https://github.com/resemble-ai/chatterbox)|An open source TTS model comparable to ElevenLabs, which supports zero-sample speech cloning and original emotional exaggeration control functions. [gemini-fullstack-langgraph-quickstart](https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-fullstack-langgraph-quickstart)|A DeepResearch framework for building production-level research assistants, encapsulating multi-step Agent workflows such as dynamic query generation, network research, and reflective analysis. [Chatterbox-TTS-Server](https://github.com/devnen/Chatterbox-TTS-Server)|An open source project for self-hosted professional-grade speech synthesis services. [gallery](https://github.com/google-ai-edge/gallery)|A Google open source AI model running application. [Windows-MCP](https://github.com/CursorTouch/Windows-MCP)|An open source MCP server that allows AI assistants to directly control Windows computers and perform system operations such as mouse clicks, dragging, moving, keyboard input, and application startup, achieving precise interface operations and true system-level AI control.
[open_deep_research](https://github.com/langchain-ai/open_deep_research)|A completely open source in-depth research agent from the LangChain team, it can perform complex research tasks on its own and automatically handle information collection, analysis and organization. [neuralagent](https://github.com/withneural/neuralagent)|An AI Agent that can complete tasks independently on the desktop. [docs](https://github.com/suitenumerique/docs)|A lightweight open source project designed to solve the pain points of team collaboration in document writing. [aisheets](https://github.com/huggingface/aisheets)|An AI tool for data set processing that provides an Excel-like table interface. You can use thousands of open source models to build, enrich, and transform data sets without coding. It supports local deployment or direct use on the Hub.
[snippai](https://github.com/xyTom/snippai)|A practical screenshot recognition AI tool that supports accurate recognition of text and formulas in pictures, analyzes image content, converts table formats and answers picture questions. [deepagents](https://github.com/hwchase17/deepagents)|An open source Python library based on the LangGraph framework for building complex AI workflows. [AionUi](https://github.com/office-sec/AionUi)|A visual chat dialogue operation interface for Gemini CLI. [resumePolice](https://github.com/itMrBoy/resumePolice)|An open source Dify workflow for resume optimization, it uses an AI evaluation system to point out resume problems, explain negative impacts and give modification suggestions to help users modify their resumes efficiently.
[gpt-5-coding-examples](https://github.com/openai/gpt-5-coding-examples)|An open source code example warehouse that demonstrates GPT-5 programming capabilities, including various types of examples such as website construction and front-end applications. [illa-helper](https://github.com/xiao-zaiyi/illa-helper)|An immersive language learning browser plug-in based on the "i+1" theory. [lazyeat](https://github.com/lanxiuyun/lazyeat)|An open source gesture control tool that solves the inconvenience of operating devices while dining. [12-factor-agents](https://github.com/humanlayer/12-factor-agents)|A guide to core design principles for developing AI Agent applications. It summarizes 12 engineering methods covering topics such as context management and tool invocation. [LunaTranslator](https://github.com/HIllya51/LunaTranslator)|A real-time translation open source tool for games without official Chinese versions. [podcastfy](https://github.com/souzatharsis/podcastfy)|An open source tool for producing podcast audio, which can extract content from web pages, PDFs, YouTube videos and pictures, and automatically generate natural and smooth podcast conversations with logic, tone and voice. [chunkr](https://github.com/lumina-ai-inc/chunkr)|A document processing open source project used to solve various document format conversion and structuring problems. It can automatically segment semantics to adapt to RAG and LLM applications, and output multiple structured formats such as HTML, Markdown, and JSON. It supports Docker deployment and Python SDK access.
[OpenBB](https://github.com/OpenBB-finance/OpenBB)|An open source data integration platform for quantitative trading and financial analysis, which provides unified access to multiple types of financial data such as stocks, options, and cryptocurrencies. [CCPlugins](https://github.com/brennercruvinel/CCPlugins)|A command extension tool specially created for Claude Code, which provides 24 carefully designed commands, which can effectively improve the quality of output results and save time on repeated work. [maestro](https://github.com/murtaza-nasir/maestro)|A fully self-hosted AI research assistant that integrates multiple AI Agents to collaboratively handle research tasks, including document library management, agent collaboration, chat-based interaction, writing assistant and real-time progress tracking functions. [zotero-arxiv-daily](https://github.com/TideDra/zotero-arxiv-daily)|An arXiv paper tracking tool based on GitHub Actions. [claude-code-cookbook](https://github.com/wasabeef/claude-code-cookbook)|A collection of configurations that enhance Claude Code's capabilities. [XCodeReviewer](https://github.com/lintsinghua/XCodeReviewer)|An intelligent code review tool that uses an original What-Why-How analysis mode to evaluate code quality from multiple dimensions such as security and performance. [blinko](https://github.com/blinko-space/blinko)|An AI-enhanced card note-taking tool designed to quickly capture and organize thought fragments. [contextgem](https://github.com/shcherbak-ai/contextgem)|An open source LLM framework that can extract structured information from documents and automatically annotate sources. [chonkie](https://github.com/chonkie-inc/chonkie)|An ultra-lightweight text chunking library that meets the needs of fast and efficient processing of various text chunking. It is only 505KB in size. It can automatically analyze market data and learn optimization strategies from historical transactions.
