[auto-subs](https://github.com/tmoroney/auto-subs)|A cross-platform audio and video subtitle generation tool that can quickly transcribe multi-language audio and video content with one click, automatically identify and mark different speakers, provide a modern subtitle editor and flexible style control, and support deep integration with DaVinci Resolve.
[claude-code-infrastructure-showcase](https://github.com/diet103/claude-code-infrastructure-showcase)|An infrastructure reference library for Claude Code, which realizes automatic activation of skills through the hooks system, uses a modular design to control the main file within 500 lines, and includes 10 professional Agents and a complete development documentation system.
[BettaFish](https://github.com/666ghj/BettaFish)|An open source public opinion analysis agent that can realize network-wide public opinion collection and in-depth analysis through natural language interaction. It supports 7x24-hour monitoring of 30+ mainstream social media platforms and short video analysis. It uses a forum collaboration mechanism to promote the collision of thinking among multiple agents to generate professional reports.
[moon-dev-ai-agents](https://github.com/moondevonyt/moon-dev-ai-agents)|An open source AI tool for stock trading strategy verification. It automatically generates and executes backtest code through natural language description. It is built based on multiple AI models such as DeepSeek. It is equipped with a multi-agent system covering strategy research, real-time trading and other aspects. It supports parallel testing of 20+ data sources and multi-model consensus decision-making.
[nof1.ai-alpha-arena](https://github.com/nof1-ai-alpha-arena/nof1.ai-alpha-arena)|An open source tool for autonomous trading of cryptocurrencies. It supports access to any large language model to drive transaction decisions, provides two modes of single-model independent analysis and multi-model collaboration, has a built-in intelligent risk control system, all data and keys are stored locally, and runs on the Hyperliquid platform based on deep reinforcement learning.
[VoiceFlow](https://github.com/infiniV/VoiceFlow)|A local speech-to-text tool based on the OpenAI Whisper model. It supports pressing and holding or switching the recording mode to input through shortcut keys. It provides more than 16 model selections, 99 language recognition and local history search. It runs completely locally without the need for an Internet connection. It is suitable for Windows 10/11 systems.
[KnowNote](https://github.com/MrSibe/KnowNote)|A local document AI-assisted reading desktop software that supports dragging in documents in multiple formats to build an exclusive local knowledge base. It can be connected to local models such as DeepSeek and Ollama for RAG Q&A, summary summarization and mind map generation. It provides an out-of-the-box installation package. The data is completely stored locally and does not require an Internet connection to run.
[clickclickclick](https://github.com/instavm/clickclickclick)|An automated control tool based on the visual capabilities of large models. It supports the use of natural language commands to automatically complete repetitive operations on Android devices and macOS computers. It is compatible with mainstream AI models and local models, without the need to write coordinate codes.
[voquill](https://github.com/josiahsrc/voquill)|A voice input tool that improves text input efficiency. It can be input directly into desktop applications through voice and is four times faster than typing. It supports cross-platform operation. It can deploy Whisper models locally or access Groq cloud services. It has AI text cleaning and personal dictionary functions.
[Raven](https://github.com/31d4r/Raven)|An open source application running based on Apple's local model, it can extract text from PDFs, pictures, audios, and videos and perform intelligent question and answer and correlation analysis. It supports macOS and iOS dual platforms, and all data is processed locally to ensure privacy.
[prism](https://github.com/qyzhg/prism)|A cross-platform AI translation tool that integrates screenshot OCR function box selection to identify translated text. It is built based on Tauri and Rust. It supports custom AI models and is compatible with the OpenAI interface. It protects privacy through SQLite local storage of translation history and provides installation packages for Windows and macOS.
[voicelive-api-salescoach](https://github.com/Azure-Samples/voicelive-api-salescoach)|An AI voice conversation simulation tool for sales training. It provides real sales scenario exercises through real-time conversations with virtual customers. After the conversation, feedback and ratings are given from multiple dimensions such as tone and content quality. It is built based on Azure Voice real-time API and GPT-4o, and supports one-click deployment to Azure or local development environment.
[UniVLA](https://github.com/OpenDriveLab/UniVLA)|An open source solution for general robot strategy learning, which unifies the action space by extracting task-centered potential actions, and uses cross-modal video data training to lower the training threshold. It only requires 5% of the computing power of OpenVLA to lead the benchmark test, and provides pre-training model library and LoRA fine-tuning support for deployment to real robots.
[Tabminal](https://github.com/Leask/Tabminal)|A browser-side open source terminal tool for mobile office developers. It supports session cloud storage and seamless cross-device work. It implements network roaming and state persistence based on WebSocket, and integrates AI error analysis, Monaco editor and mobile virtual HHKB keyboard layout.
[ebook-mcp](https://github.com/onebirdrocks/ebook-mcp)|An open source project that solves the "last mile" problem of dialogue between local book libraries and AI. It allows AI tools such as Cursor or Claude to directly "read" local e-books. It supports EPUB and PDF formats, and converts e-book content into Markdown format that is easy for AI to understand. It is lightweight and relies on Python and uv package managers.
[VideoPipe](https://github.com/sherlockchou86/VideoPipe)|An open source framework for building video analysis applications. It supports complete functions such as video reading and decoding, target tracking, and behavior analysis through node combination. It provides more than 40 prototype examples. It is written based on C++ and has few dependencies and is easy to transplant. It adopts pipeline design and is compatible with multiple inference backends.
[cocoindex](https://github.com/cocoindex-io/cocoindex)|A high-performance data conversion framework specially built for AI scenarios. It can define the entire process from file reading, chunking to vector storage through about 100 lines of Python code. It supports multiple data sources and targets, has built-in common conversion components such as text chunking and embedding generation, and provides more than 20 examples of practical application scenarios.
[sokuji](https://github.com/kizuna-ai-lab/sokuji)|An open source real-time speech translation tool that is connected to mainstream large models such as OpenAI and Google Gemini. It captures microphone input in real time and plays the translated speech. It provides a desktop version and browser plug-in to adapt to conference scenarios, and supports Linux virtual audio equipment and audio visualization.
[nexa-sdk](https://github.com/NexaAI/nexa-sdk)|A localized AI solution for personal devices that supports rapid processing of local data in disconnected environments, such as searching thousands of photos within milliseconds, using self-developed model compression technology.
[agentic-rag-for-dummies](https://github.com/GiovanniPasq/agentic-rag-for-dummies)|A production-grade Agentic RAG solution built on LangGraph. It supports dialogue memory and hierarchical indexing mechanisms to solve out-of-context problems. Multiple agents can process complex problems in parallel and proactively ask questions to confirm intentions. It has built-in Gradio interactive interface and Docker deployment, and provides PDF to Markdown guides and tool comparisons.
[paperai](https://github.com/neuml/paperai)|An open source tool designed for large-scale literature retrieval and analysis. It uses AI to convert documents into a knowledge base, supports batch questions and generates in-depth reports. Combined with LLM and RAG technology, it can accurately mark answers on the original PDF file. It provides one-click deployment of Docker images and Python environment installation, and the data processing process is clear and controllable.
[claude-code-system-prompts](https://github.com/Piebald-AI/claude-code-system-prompts)|An open source project that demonstrates the working mechanism of Claude Code and discloses its system prompt word files and version change logs, including the evolution process of more than 40 files including main prompt words, sub-agent prompt words, etc. and 52 versions.
[SciToolAgent](https://github.com/HICAI-ZJU/SciToolAgent)|An AI tool that assists scientific research. It integrates more than 500 scientific tools by building a scientific tool knowledge graph. It uses a "planning-execution-summarization" architecture to break down complex tasks and execute them. It has a security check mechanism to monitor experimental risks. It is developed based on Python and supports Conda quick configuration and custom addition of private tools.
[Office-Word-MCP-Server](https://github.com/GongRzhe/Office-Word-MCP-Server)|An open source MCP server that allows AI to directly operate Word documents, from creation, typesetting to table generation in one go. It also supports reading existing documents, extracting comments and global format adjustment. It can be connected to Claude Desktop through Smithery one-click installation or Python local deployment.
[promptomatix](https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/promptomatix)|An automated framework for prompt word optimization that can analyze task requirements, automatically generate training data and iteratively optimize prompt words. It supports multiple model providers and command line and API usage, and provides detailed tutorials.
[vibe-coding-cn](https://github.com/tukuaiai/vibe-coding-cn)|A practical guide for AI-assisted programming, which provides standardized system prompt words, project directory templates and full-link scripts. It guides AI to generate maintainable code through planning-first methodology, and includes multi-scenario prompt words, meta-prompt word generator and Skills skill library.
[FluidVoice](https://github.com/altic-dev/FluidVoice)|An open source speech-to-text tool for Apple Silicon chip Macs. It supports voice input and control of Mac operations. It implements real-time transcription based on the Parakeet TDT v3 model and can be connected to the AI ​​model to optimize recognition and polish text. It requires macOS 14.0 and above.
[Open-AutoGLM](https://github.com/zai-org/Open-AutoGLM)|An open source project that uses multi-modal large models to realize automatic control of mobile phones. It can identify complex UIs, simulate click input, and complete cross-application long-link tasks. It has been adapted to more than 50 mainstream applications. All model weights and automated operation frameworks are open source and support private deployment by developers.
[open-scouts](https://github.com/firecrawl/open-scouts)|An open source information monitoring tool built on Next.js 16 and Supabase. It supports custom monitoring tasks and runs automatically at scheduled times. It uses AI intelligent analysis to analyze search results to generate briefings and push them via email. It integrates Firecrawl web crawling function and supports Docker one-click deployment.
[agents.md](https://github.com/agentsmd/agents.md)|A project configuration file that provides exclusive operation guidelines for AI programming agents. It separates human-oriented documents from machine-oriented construction steps, test instructions, and code specifications. It is compatible with mainstream AI programming tools, supports nested use of large projects, and has low configuration costs.
[ScholarXIV](https://github.com/dagmawibabi/ScholarXIV)|An arXiv paper reading application specially optimized for mobile terminals. It integrates search, download, collection and reading functions. It is connected to the Gemini model to support AI dialogue to summarize the core of the paper or explain the difficult points. It provides an Android installation package with a minimalist and beautiful interface and supports dark mode.
[banana-slides](https://github.com/Anionex/banana-slides)|An AI slide generation tool dedicated to balancing efficiency and beauty. It supports conversational natural language editing to adjust layout elements in real time. It can upload PDF, Markdown and other files to parse materials and export editable files with one click. It provides an open source solution for one-click deployment of Docker Compose.
[Paper2Slides](https://github.com/HKUDS/Paper2Slides)|An open source tool that can convert paper reports into professional slides and posters. It supports multiple formats such as PDF and Word. It provides a variety of built-in themes and natural language description styles. It automatically extracts key chart data and traces the source based on search enhancement generation technology. It has fast preview, breakpoint resume and parallel generation functions. It can be used through the command line and web pages.
[openskills](https://github.com/numman-ali/openskills)|An open source tool that transplants the Claude Code skill system to multiple platforms. It replicates its complete skill mechanism through the command line. It supports the installation of dozens of skills such as PDF processing and Excel editing from official and GitHub repositories. It is suitable for AI programming assistants such as Cursor and Windsurf.
[PromptFill](https://github.com/TanShilongMario/PromptFill)|A structured prompt word generation tool designed for AI painting. It quickly builds and manages complex prompts through visual fill-in-the-blank interaction. It supports custom word library classification, drag-and-drop editing, preview switching and two-way synchronization of new keywords. All modifications are saved locally and no complex configuration is required.
[all-agentic-architectures](https://github.com/FareedKhan-dev/all-agentic-architectures)|A practical open source project for modern AI agent design, including 17 mainstream Agent architectures, covering modes from basic tool invocation to advanced multi-agent collaboration. Using LangGraph for orchestration, it provides runnable Jupyter Notebooks and quantitative evaluation mechanisms to help developers understand the underlying logic and master high-level development paradigms.
[easy-llm-cli](https://github.com/ConardLi/easy-llm-cli)|An AI tool command line interface that runs on the terminal. It is compatible with various large models and local models such as Gemini, OpenAI, Claude, etc. It supports expansion capabilities through MCP and reading the code library context. It can automatically perform Git operations, refactor code or generate applications. It can be run without installation through npx based on the Node.js environment.
[reddit-mcp-buddy](https://github.com/karanb192/reddit-mcp-buddy)|A Reddit browsing MCP server built for AI assistants, giving AI the ability to search and browse Reddit in real time. It supports viewing hot lists, searching for topics, analyzing user posting history, and grabbing comment areas. It provides npm and docker deployment methods, has a built-in intelligent caching mechanism, and data processing is completed locally.
[claude-mem](https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem)|A persistent memory tool for Claude Code to solve the problem of session restart amnesia. It automatically captures operation details and tool calls and generates semantic summary storage, supports natural language query of project history, uses a progressive disclosure strategy to save tokens, and is equipped with a local web interface and privacy control mechanism.
[n8n-skills](https://github.com/czlonkowski/n8n-skills)|An open source project that injects core skills into Claude Code. It has a built-in n8n development specification knowledge base to implement expression syntax correction, node configuration verification, Webhook data structure identification, and guide production-level JavaScript code writing, effectively solving the problem of AI-generated node parameter errors.
[chatgpt-md](https://github.com/bramses/chatgpt-md)|An AI integration plug-in for Obsidian that supports initiating conversations directly within notes and referencing other notes as context. It can seamlessly connect to ChatGPT, OpenRouter platform models and local models running through Ollama, etc. The conversation content is completely saved locally and supports Markdown rendering and independent configuration of model parameters.
[inksight](https://github.com/google-research/inksight)|A Google open-source handwritten note conversion system can convert handwritten note photos into vector handwriting that retains writing traces and supports editing and search. Based on the visual converter and mT5 encoding and decoding architecture, it achieves content understanding and writing process restoration through dual training.
[OpenContracts](https://github.com/Open-Source-Legal/OpenContracts)|An open source document analysis platform designed for knowledge-intensive work. It supports the uploading, annotation and analysis of PDF and text format documents, provides enterprise-level functions such as batch data extraction, custom metadata schema, collaborative annotation and permission management. It builds a language model system based on the PydanticAI framework and quickly deploys it through Docker.
[Nano-PDF](https://github.com/gavrielc/Nano-PDF)|A natural language-based PDF slideshow editor that supports updating dates, adjusting data, correcting typos and generating new slides through text commands. It uses the Google Gemini 3 Pro Image model and can process multiple pages in parallel and retain text layers. A paid Gemini API key is required.
[MLE-agent](https://github.com/MLSysOps/MLE-agent)|An AI intelligent assistant designed specifically for machine learning engineers. It can automatically build ML baseline solutions based on fuzzy requirements and independently complete the entire Kaggle competition process. It integrates Arxiv and Papers with Code resources, supports intelligent debugging, automatically generates weekly reports, provides an interactive command line chat interface, and can access multiple AI models.
[obs-localvocal](https://github.com/royshil/obs-localvocal)|A localized speech-to-text plug-in for OBS that converts speech into high-quality subtitles in real time and supports simultaneous translation. No need to connect to the Internet or pay API fees. It is built based on the ggml and Whisper models. It is simple to configure and the model size can be customized according to the computer configuration to balance speed and accuracy.
[Local-File-Organizer](https://github.com/QiuYannnn/Local-File-Organizer)|An intelligent tool that uses large model capabilities to organize local files. It uses AI to understand file content and automatically completes renaming, classification, archiving, and duplicate cleaning. It supports access to Ollama to run local models to ensure privacy and security, and can also configure cloud APIs such as DeepSeek or Kimi.
[smol-podcaster](https://github.com/FanaHOVA/smol-podcaster)|An open source productivity tool for podcast creators that can automate post-processing processes, including generating verbatim transcripts to distinguish speakers, extracting chapters with timestamps, creating titles and publicity copy, solving audio and video out-of-sync issues and automatically aligning timestamps. The data is saved locally by default to ensure privacy and security.
[argos-translate](https://github.com/argosopentech/argos-translate)|An open source offline translation library based on Python that supports translation between more than 40 languages, including Chinese, English and other commonly used languages. When there is no direct model, it can be translated through an intermediate language. Local processing of data ensures privacy and is available as a Python library, command line tool and visual interface, with support for GPU acceleration and translation of HTML and document files.
[superpowers](https://github.com/obra/superpowers)|A skill library that provides a systematic workflow for Claude Code, including testing, debugging, collaboration, development and other functions. It adopts a test-driven development process, a systematic four-step method for debugging, automatic verification before submission, and supports slash commands such as brainstorming and planning. Skills can be automatically activated according to tasks.
[Paper2All](https://github.com/YuhangChen1/Paper2All)|An open source tool for generating academic promotional materials. Upload a paper PDF to automatically generate interactive websites, academic posters, promotional videos and social media copywriting. It supports custom poster sizes, digital human explanation videos and multi-platform style tweets. It can be deployed locally and is compatible with model APIs such as OpenAI or OpenRouter.
[RedInk](https://github.com/HisMax/RedInk)|A Xiaohongshu image and text generator based on Nano Banana Pro. It can batch generate 6-9 pages of images and texts with a unified style by inputting topics and reference images. It supports editing content outlines, adjusting descriptors and regenerating individually. It can be deployed and configured through Docker with Gemini or OpenAI compatible APIs, and provides a visual web interface and high concurrency mode.
[WhisperLiveKit](https://github.com/QuentinFuxa/WhisperLiveKit)|A local real-time speech recognition and translation solution that optimizes streaming transmission latency, supports high-precision transcription, speaker recognition and voice activity detection. It can be connected to multiple speech engines and integrates NLLW models to achieve translation in 200 languages.

### Learning Tutorials

Project|Description
---|---
[90DaysOfCyberSecurity](https://github.com/farhanashrafdev/90DaysOfCyberSecurity)|A network security system learning plan, covering topics such as network basics, Linux, Python, traffic analysis, cloud services, and hacker attacks. It provides a structured learning path to help users systematically master network security knowledge and skills in multiple fields.
[data-engineer-handbook](https://github.com/DataExpert-io/data-engineer-handbook)|A learning manual to help you become an outstanding data engineer. It covers books, courses, interview materials, excellent blogs, communities and bloggers worth following, etc., and provides a learning roadmap for junior data engineers.
[grammar-club](https://github.com/llwslc/grammar-club)|This is an open source and free online English grammar learning book. It adopts a step-by-step arrangement. It is divided into three sections and 22 chapters from elementary simple sentences to advanced simplified clauses. It supports online reading.
[self-llm](https://github.com/datawhalechina/self-llm)|An open source large model usage guide for domestic beginners. It provides an open source LLM environment configuration tutorial based on the Linux platform, simplifies the deployment, use and application process of the model, and gives detailed guidance for different model requirements.
[50projects50days](https://github.com/bradtraversy/50projects50days)|A list of practice projects suitable for front-end beginners, including 50 small demos implemented in pure HTML, CSS and JavaScript, covering common small website functions such as animated navigation, background sliders, password generators, and image carousels.
[machine-learning-list](https://github.com/elicit/machine-learning-list)|A list of tutorials for machine learning novices and advanced learners, covering a complete knowledge system from basic introduction to advanced applications, including training fine-tuning, inference strategies, data set processing and practical applications.
[LinuxNetworkProgramming](https://github.com/nguyenchiemminhvu/LinuxNetworkProgramming)|A learning guide for Linux network programming, covering comprehensive content from basic socket programming to using libcurl and OpenSSL for secure communication, including the basic knowledge of Linux network programming and the use and implementation of Socket API.
[LLMForEverybody](https://github.com/luhengshiwo/LLMForEverybody)|A large model knowledge learning material for the public, covering pre-training, deployment inference, fine-tuning, quantification, prompt word engineering, agents, enterprise implementation and evaluation indicators, etc., suitable for interview preparation for large language model-related positions.
[LLMBook-zh.github.io](https://github.com/LLMBook-zh/LLMBook-zh.github.io)|An e-book for introductory readers of large models, providing a detailed explanation of large models and the overall technical framework and roadmap, covering the basic theoretical part, including the architecture and basic technologies of large models.
[WTF-zk](https://github.com/WTFAcademy/WTF-zk)|An introductory tutorial on zero-knowledge proof for those with basic knowledge of high school mathematics. It helps learners master zk knowledge by reproducing relevant algorithms in Python and lowers the entry threshold for zero-knowledge proof technology.
[97](https://github.com/ninehills/blog/issues/97)|A summary of large language model learning resources, divided into introductory, application and in-depth chapters, covering Prompt prompt project, Langchain framework, introduction to deep learning, model theory and practice and fine-tuning training, etc., providing relevant learning materials and videos.
[qwerty-learner](https://github.com/RealKai42/qwerty-learner)|A tool that integrates word memorization and typing practice. It has built-in vocabulary libraries such as GET-4, GMAT, GRE, Postgraduate Entrance Examination English and common English words for programmers. Through keyboard input, you can practice typing while memorizing words. It includes phonetic symbol display and pronunciation functions, provides silent writing mode and can count typing performance.
[100-exercises-to-learn-rust](https://github.com/mainmatter/100-exercises-to-learn-rust)|An open source project that helps developers quickly master the Rust language. It systematically learns Rust's syntax, system types, standard library usage and ecosystem through 100 exercises. It provides GitHub warehouse and Chinese version learning resources.
[operating-system-in-1000-lines](https://github.com/nuta/operating-system-in-1000-lines)|A tutorial on building a micro-operating system from scratch. It gradually implements functions such as context switching, paging, user mode, command line shell, disk drive, and file reading and writing through 17 chapters. It only requires 1,000 lines of code based on C language and all codes are open source.
[smol-course](https://github.com/huggingface/smol-course)|A tutorial on the use of small language model alignment officially produced by HuggingFace. Covering instruction tuning, preference adjustment, parameter efficient fine-tuning, evaluation, visual language models, synthetic data sets, inference and capstone projects, etc., based on the SmolLM2 series model teaching, it is suitable for fine-tuning and training language models to specific fields.
[docker-tutorial](https://github.com/jaywcjlove/docker-tutorial)|An introductory tutorial for Docker beginners, covering basic introduction, installation and use, service/image/container management, private warehouse construction and usage practice, which can help users quickly master the relevant knowledge and skills of Docker deployment applications.
[pytorch-deep-learning](https://github.com/mrdbourke/pytorch-deep-learning)|A deep learning online tutorial for beginners with basic knowledge of Python and machine learning. It covers 9 chapters including basic knowledge of PyTorch, neural networks, and computer vision. It provides video tutorials, practice demos, and open source code to support theoretical learning and practical operations.
[train-deepseek-r1](https://github.com/FareedKhan-dev/train-deepseek-r1)|An open source project that implements the DeepSeek R1 training process from scratch. It displays the training process through hand-drawn flowcharts and code analysis, including GRPO algorithm, SFT and inference-oriented reinforcement learning implementation, and uses a small basic model to support local training.
[Deepdive-llama3-from-scratch](https://github.com/therealoliver/Deepdive-llama3-from-scratch)|A GitHub tutorial for learning the Llama 3 model to help understand the implementation principles and reasoning process. It has a clear directory structure to facilitate step-by-step understanding of the code and a large number of detailed code comments.
[makeMoE](https://github.com/AviSoori1x/makeMoE)|A tutorial on implementing a sparse MoE model from scratch. It introduces the basic composition of the MoE model and demonstrates the code implementation of each component. It provides examples of model initialization, training loops and text generation, emphasizing training stability and efficiency optimization.
[README.md](https://github.com/agno-agi/agno/blob/main/cookbook/getting_started/README.md)|A step-by-step tutorial on building AI agents, using 19 step-by-step in-depth examples to build AI agents with various functions from scratch. Each example includes detailed comments, prompts and required dependencies.
[earthworm](https://github.com/cuixueshe/earthworm)|An open source project to help learn English. It uses conjunctions to form sentences and a step-by-step method to train sentences from simple to complex. It combines game rewards and point ranking mechanisms to improve the fun and efficiency of memorizing words, and forms muscle memory through repetition.
[MCP-Chinese-Getting-Started-Guide](https://github.com/liaokongVFX/MCP-Chinese-Getting-Started-Guide)|A quick introductory tutorial on MCP programming. It explains step by step from developing and debugging MCP servers to combining DeepSeek + Cline to build graphic and text generation examples to help users gain an in-depth understanding of MCP.
[stanford-cme-295-transformers-large-language-models](https://github.com/afshinea/stanford-cme-295-transformers-large-language-models)|A learning material for AI developers and students, which condenses the core concepts and technical points of Transformer and large language models into intuitive illustrations, which can help quickly master the key concepts related to large models.
[DevOps-Roadmap](https://github.com/milanm/DevOps-Roadmap)|A systematic DevOps learning resource integration roadmap, covering a comprehensive knowledge system from Git, programming languages, Linux to cloud services, including a large number of learning resource links and detailed study guides for 12 key skills, helping to systematically learn DevOps skills.
[overseas-website-note](https://github.com/princehuang/overseas-website-note)|A guide document on the collection process for overseas business, which explains in detail the whole process of company registration, EIN application, bank card processing, Stripe activation and payment collection, etc., and provides a closed-loop collection solution for users who plan to expand overseas business.
[building-llm-applications-from-scratch](https://github.com/hamzafarooq/building-llm-applications-from-scratch)|An open source course for engineers, data scientists and AI researchers with Python and basic machine learning knowledge. It teaches the ability to design, build and deploy customized LLM solutions without relying on ready-made frameworks. It provides an in-depth explanation of the basic components of the RAG system, covering six major weeks of natural language processing basics, Transformer and LLM system design.
[FAQ_Of_LLM_Interview](https://github.com/aceliuchanghong/FAQ_Of_LLM_Interview)|A collection of interview questions for job seekers in large model algorithm positions, covering the basics of large model application, optimization technology, distributed training, efficient fine-tuning, etc., providing a full range of knowledge points from basic concepts to practical applications.
[PromptJailbreakManual](https://github.com/Acmesec/PromptJailbreakManual)|A systematic and comprehensive Prompt jailbreak manual, which explains in detail the construction essence and thinking mode of Prompt, and provides rich attack and defense examples and framework templates to help solve various challenges in the use of AI.
[langgraph-101](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph-101)|A tutorial on learning the basics of LangGraph designed by the LangChain team. A series of notebooks help you master the LangGraph framework, which is designed to build precise and controllable AI agents and multi-agent applications. It contains six chapters and provides a complete environment construction guide and Azure OpenAI integration solution. The case code has been open source.
[Grokking-the-Coding-Interview-Patterns-for-Coding-Questions](https://github.com/dipjul/Grokking-the-Coding-Interview-Patterns-for-Coding-Questions)|A collection of algorithm questions for programming interviews. 28 core algorithms are classified according to problem-solving modes, covering types such as double pointers and sliding windows. It provides detailed introductions, practice questions of different difficulty, and links to questions on platforms such as LeetCode.
[drawio-desktop](https://github.com/jgraph/drawio-desktop)|An open source tool for drawing professional diagrams. It supports the creation of various complex diagrams such as flow charts and UML class diagrams. It has rich drawing elements and can save files to a personal cloud disk or locally to ensure data privacy and security.
[ai-by-hand-excel](https://github.com/ImagineAILab/ai-by-hand-excel)|An Excel teaching tool for manual implementation of AI models, helping learners understand the working principles from basic algorithms to complex deep learning architectures through practical operations, including neural network practice sets from basic algorithms such as Softmax and LeakyReLU to deep learning architectures.
[LLMs-from-scratch-CN](https://github.com/MLNLP-World/LLMs-from-scratch-CN)|A guide to building large language models for beginners, leading readers to create a GPT-like language model from scratch, covering core aspects such as model creation, training and fine-tuning, and systematically explaining the construction process of large language models (LLMs) through 8 chapters.
[english-note](https://github.com/hzpt-inet-club/english-note)|An open source study note for those who are weak in basic English grammar. It systematically explains English words, sentence components, sentence patterns and other knowledge. It uses vivid and interesting illustrations to help understand complex and boring grammatical content.
[tiny-llm](https://github.com/skyzh/tiny-llm)|An open source tutorial on LLM construction for Mac users. The professor starts from basic mathematical operations and gradually builds a large model. It is based on MLX implementation, adopts a three-week learning plan and does not rely on complex advanced interfaces.
[cs-self-learning](https://github.com/PKUFlyingPig/cs-self-learning)|A systematic and comprehensive computer science self-study guide, covering dozens of fields such as programming languages, algorithms, operating systems, and artificial intelligence. [spring2025-lectures](https://github.com/stanford-cs336/spring2025-lectures)|A Stanford University open course for large model developers, building a complete large language model from scratch, covering key aspects such as word segmentation, model architecture, GPU optimization, parallel training, data processing, model alignment, etc., including 5 programming assignments and video teaching with open source sample code.
[claude-code-cheat-sheet](https://github.com/Njengah/claude-code-cheat-sheet)|A quick reference manual for Claude Code learners, providing a learning path from scratch to mastery, dividing functions into 10 levels according to difficulty, including detailed command examples, usage scenarios, command reference tables, best practices and solutions to common problems.
[aws-devops-zero-to-hero](https://github.com/iam-veeramalla/aws-devops-zero-to-hero)|A 30-day AWS learning plan specially designed for DevOps engineers. Learn a core AWS service every day, including theoretical knowledge, practical projects, interview questions and real cases, with complete YouTube video tutorials, covering best practices and optimization suggestions from basics to advanced.
[image-classification-with-local-vlms](https://github.com/Paulescu/image-classification-with-local-vlms)|An open source tutorial for edge AI development and mobile model deployment developers. It provides step-by-step instructions on how to build a high-precision and fast-response image classifier on mobile phones. Taking cat and dog classification as a starting point, it explains the use of local visual language models for image classification, including model evaluation, prompt word optimization, L oRA fine-tuning and other knowledge, using structured generation technology to increase the model accuracy from 73% to 98%, and then LoRA fine-tuning to maintain lightweight and achieve 100% accuracy, and finally deployed to iOS applications to achieve offline recognition, using Python and Modal platforms for development.
[agentic-design-patterns-cn](https://github.com/ginobefun/agentic-design-patterns-cn)|A Chinese-English translation material for "Agentic Design Patterns", which systematically organizes 21 agent design patterns, covering from basic prompt chain to advanced multi-agent collaboration. It provides detailed implementation methods, code examples and online running environments, and builds learning paths according to the difficulty.
[CS7038-Malware-Analysis](https://github.com/ckane/CS7038-Malware-Analysis)|A university malware analysis course that provides a systematic learning path from zero to mastery. It covers a complete course system, rich lecture videos, practical experimental assignments and detailed tutorials on tools. It is classified and indexed by subject. It requires basic operating systems and data structures and a multi-virtual machine operating environment.
[Machine-Learning-Specialization-Coursera](https://github.com/greyhatguy007/Machine-Learning-Specialization-Coursera)|A collection of learning materials from Andrew Ng's machine learning course, which fully contains the content of the three courses, including detailed explanations of core algorithms, answers to exercises and programming assignments, actual project cases and recommendations for basic mathematics courses. All materials are in Jupyter Notebook format and can be run directly locally.
[image-classification-with-local-vlms](https://github.com/Paulescu/image-classification-with-local-vlms)|An open source free tutorial for beginners in edge AI development, guiding the deployment of high-precision local image classification systems on edge devices such as mobile phones. The LFM2-VL model based on Liquid AI provides progressive practical teaching from cat and dog classification to car brand recognition, covering complete workflows such as model evaluation, LoRA fine-tuning and iOS application deployment.
[lerobot](https://github.com/huggingface/lerobot)|A free course for robotics learners, providing a complete learning system from entry to practice, covering core theories such as kinematics, dynamics, and control systems, combined with practical projects such as deep learning applications, robotic arm control, and autonomous navigation, as well as introductions to ROS and PyBullet tools, and rich Python code implementation.
[bRAG-langchain](https://github.com/bRAGAI/bRAG-langchain)|A RAG technology learning project, including 5 Jupyter notebooks, covering the complete learning path from infrastructure construction to advanced technology implementation. By explaining in detail practical technologies such as multi-query, route distribution, and reordering, and providing complete and executable code examples, it helps developers systematically master the core RAG technology.
[cs249r_book](https://github.com/harvard-edge/cs249r_book)|An open source textbook on machine learning system engineering. It teaches the knowledge of building implementable AI systems, covering the complete technology stack from system design, data engineering to model deployment and edge AI, and provides practical projects and hands-on experiments.
[leetgpu-challenges](https://github.com/AlphaGPU/leetgpu-challenges)|A set of challenge questions for GPU programming learners, providing systematic questions of different difficulty levels. Each question includes detailed problem descriptions, examples and constraints, and is equipped with reference implementations, complete test cases and starting template codes for various GPU programming frameworks.
[project-box](https://github.com/dexter-xD/project-box)|A collection of projects for C language beginners, providing a complete learning path from entry to advanced, including 10 projects of different difficulty, from basic tic-tac-toe games to complex network programming. Each project has complete source code, build files and usage instructions.
[EasyOffer](https://github.com/jingtian11/EasyOffer)|An open source practical guide for large model algorithm job interviews. Provides mainstream LLM handwritten code implementation, module analysis and interview records, including the core structure of the DeepSeek series, generation method code, DPO training implementation and common interview coding questions.
[c](https://github.com/little-book-of/c)|A C language learning manual for programming beginners, focusing on teaching basic syntax, core concepts and practical skills. Provides various formats such as PDF, EPUB, and online web pages, including LaTeX source code to support custom content, and the sample codes are all open source.
[Book-Mathematical-Foundation-of-Reinforcement-Learning](https://github.com/MathFoundationRL/Book-Mathematical-Foundation-of-Reinforcement-Learning)|An open source book for reinforcement learning learners, providing a clear path to understanding RL from mathematical principles. Through the "Grid World" case throughout, it explains the mathematical principles behind the algorithm in simple terms and controls the depth of mathematics.
[how-to-build-a-coding-agent](https://github.com/ghuntley/how-to-build-a-coding-agent)|An open source tutorial project that helps developers build AI programming assistants from scratch. It gradually adds functions such as file reading and command execution through 6 practical examples. It provides open source code, detailed step instructions, architecture diagrams, and flow charts to analyze the working principle of the coding agent.
[Cookbook](https://github.com/andkret/Cookbook)|An open source study guide for data engineering learners. Provides a systematic learning route from the basics of Linux and Docker to the core technologies of Spark and Kafka, including real case analysis of major manufacturers, over 1,000 interview questions, free data sets and practical tutorials.
[vibe-coding](https://github.com/EnzeD/vibe-coding)|A practical guide for AI-assisted programming, which provides a complete methodology from project initialization to debugging and repair. It guides AI to maintain a clear and consistent code base structure through the concept of planning first and the Memory Bank mechanism. It includes step-by-step implementation strategies and tool usage guides.
[interview-guide](https://github.com/nas5w/interview-guide)|A technical interview preparation guide for software engineers. It refines personal methodology based on nearly a hundred interview experiences. It focuses on the interview preparation process instead of stacking resources. The content is easy to understand and highly operable.
[open-source-cs](https://github.com/ForrestKnight/open-source-cs)|A list of tutorials that systematically organizes computer science knowledge, including free online courses from top universities such as MIT and Stanford. According to the classification of the traditional computer undergraduate course system, it covers core knowledge such as programming, mathematics, and systems. Each course is marked with credit hours, difficulty, and prerequisites, making it suitable for self-learners to study systematically.
[MachineLearningDemos](https://github.com/GeostatsGuy/MachineLearningDemos)|A free open source e-book for systematically learning machine learning. It provides complete mathematical derivation and pure Python manual implementation of 30+ algorithms, combined with interactive visualization tools to intuitively display the training process and parameter changes, and is accompanied by YouTube video lectures and code warehouses.
[Cybersecurity-Mastery-Roadmap](https://github.com/Hamed233/Cybersecurity-Mastery-Roadmap)|A study guide for network security learners, providing a complete learning path from zero basics to security experts. It is mainly divided into five stages. The first stage covers computer basics and other content.
[evaluation-guidebook](https://github.com/huggingface/evaluation-guidebook)|A systematic guide to LLM assessment. Covering the three mainstream methods of automated benchmark testing, manual evaluation and LLM-as-a-judge, it provides practical experience, troubleshooting skills and evaluation plan design. The content is layered by difficulty and supports Chinese and French community translation versions.
[apps.apple.com](https://github.com/rxliuli/apps.apple.com)|An open source production code project for the new version of Apple's App Store webpage, including a complete component library, state management, API integration, routing configuration, etc., built based on Svelte and TypeScript.
[The-Accidental-CTO](https://github.com/subhashchy/The-Accidental-CTO)|An open source book for engineers, architects and technology entrepreneurs. Share the real experience of a non-scientific CTO in expanding the platform from zero to supporting millions of users, and explain the core concepts of distributed systems, technical decision-making trade-offs and trade-offs through story-based cases, covering architecture evolution, technology selection, observability, etc.
[AI-Engineering.academy](https://github.com/adithya-s-k/AI-Engineering.academy)|A systematic learning roadmap for beginners of AI technology. It covers six core modules: prompt engineering, RAG system, model fine-tuning, production deployment, AI agent and practical projects. Each module covers basic concepts to project practice, emphasizing the combination of theory and practice, and provides free online learning.
[lovable-for-beginners](https://github.com/cporter202/lovable-for-beginners)|An open source tutorial for programming beginners to develop websites through AI conversation without writing code. Based on the "Vibe Coding" concept, you can build a website by describing requirements in natural language. It includes complete modules and practical exercises from project startup to deployment and launch. It also teaches prompt word engineering and application debugging and testing methods.
[chroma-cookbooks](https://github.com/chroma-core/chroma-cookbooks)|An open source project for AI application developers, providing complete code examples and guides from data embedding and retrieval to complex RAG process construction, supporting the use of Chroma Cloud and local development logic reference.
[tensor.h](https://github.com/apoorvnandan/tensor.h)|A teaching project to help understand the underlying logic of neural networks, using pure C language to implement a micro tensor library from scratch, covering tensor operations, automatic derivation, forward and backward propagation and complete training process, with detailed code and mathematical derivation.
[github-copilot-for-data-science](https://github.com/microsoft/github-copilot-for-data-science)|A Microsoft open source GitHub Copilot data science tutorial teaches users to use Copilot's advanced functions to improve efficiency. It can complete data processing, chart generation and report export in Jupyter Notebook. It supports custom agents and is based on real project scenarios and resources can be directly practiced.
[vibe-vibe](https://github.com/datawhalechina/vibe-vibe)|An AI-assisted programming guide for people with different programming basics. It uses natural language to describe requirements to allow AI to generate code. The zero-based entry part starts with basic concepts and the full-stack practical part is based on Next.js+TypeScript+Prisma for systematic learning.
[github-copilot-for-data-science](https://github.com/microsoft/github-copilot-for-data-science)|A tutorial for data scientists, based on the advanced functions of GitHub Copilot to improve the efficiency of data analysis, covering the complete process of data cleaning, visualization and report generation. It is implemented through slash commands, inline chat and @workspace agents. It is equipped with actual project data and dependent environments. You only need a GitHub account to practice.
[Quant-Developers-Resources](https://github.com/cybergeekgyan/Quant-Developers-Resources)|An open source entry guide for quantitative job seekers and financial engineering learners, covering core knowledge points from mathematical foundations, stochastic calculus, programming languages ​​to algorithmic trading and classified according to interview requirements. It includes a list of master's video courses and recruitment lists of major hedge funds and high-frequency trading companies.
[hello-agents](https://github.com/datawhalechina/hello-agents)|An open source tutorial on AI agent development for developers and students with basic Python skills, from basic theory to complete construction of multi-agent systems, covering core technologies such as memory systems, context engineering, multi-agent communication protocols, and reinforcement learning training, with comprehensive cases, complete codes, and exercise designs.
[Books](https://github.com/AniruddhaChattopadhyay/Books)|A collection of AI and machine learning open source books on GitHub, which brings together 13 classic books that can be shared for free, covering core and cutting-edge topics such as deep learning interview question sets and the basics of large language models. Each book is equipped with a short introduction and supports direct preview or download to local reading.
[MedLLMsPracticalGuide](https://github.com/AI-in-Health/MedLLMsPracticalGuide)|A practical guide for learners of large medical models, combing through the entire process from model construction to application, covering mainstream model architecture, core medical data sets and downstream tasks, and discussing technical challenges and future evolution directions.
[fudoki](https://github.com/iamcheyan/fudoki)|An open source tool for Japanese language learners that can visually analyze Japanese text structure and provide natural reading function. Based on the Kuromoji.js word segmentation engine, it marks parts of speech, kana and romanization. It supports Markdown editing, multi-document management and local deployment. You can view dictionary definitions and adjust the speaking speed and timbre.
[project-box](https://github.com/dexter-xD/project-box)|A collection of practical projects for C language beginners, including 10 independent projects ranging from easy to difficult, covering algorithm implementation, network programming, compiler design and game development. Each project is equipped with complete documentation and build system.
[qxresearch-event-1](https://github.com/qxresearch/qxresearch-event-1)|A minimalist code project for Python learners, it contains more than 50 practical applications with about 10 lines of code, covering functions such as PDF processing, GUI development, ChatGPT integration, etc. It is equipped with video explanations and simple environment configuration, suitable for novices to practice and veterans to accumulate script libraries.
[tech-vault](https://github.com/moabukar/tech-vault)|A technical interview preparation question bank compiled by experienced engineers, covering core areas such as Linux, DevOps, AWS, and system design. It focuses on solving practical engineering problems and provides a command line question brushing tool. The content is open source and continuously updated.
[101-linux-commands](https://github.com/bobbyiliev/101-linux-commands)|An open source e-book for systematically mastering Linux command line operations. It is developed step by step from basic to advanced through 101 practical commands. Each command is equipped with detailed descriptions, practical usage examples and application scenarios, covering various practical scenarios such as file management and text processing. It also provides multiple format reading and local operation support.
[algs4](https://github.com/landerrosette/algs4)|A GitHub project that re-implements the core content of "Algorithms (4th Edition)" in C++. It covers classic algorithms in six major modules such as sorting, search, and graph theory. Each algorithm is equipped with a test client and supports CMake one-click or separate compilation for easy verification and review.

### Utility Tools

Project|Description
---|---
[MyIP](https://github.com/jason5ng32/MyIP)|A completely open source out-of-the-box IP toolbox that can easily check network IP related information, including IP geolocation, DNS leaks, speed test, Ping test and website availability, while supporting dark mode, responsive interface, multi-language and shortcut key functions.
[czkawka](https://github.com/qarmin/czkawka)|A powerful cross-platform file cleaning tool that supports scanning for duplicates, empty folders, large files, similar media and invalid files. It has high scanning efficiency. It is suitable for Linux, Windows, macOS systems and supports multiple languages ​​​​such as Chinese.
[stats](https://github.com/exelban/stats)|A macOS system monitoring tool that can be used as an open source alternative to iStat Menus. It supports monitoring the usage of CPU, GPU, disk, running memory, network, etc. It can also check battery usage and control fans and Bluetooth devices.
[web-check](https://github.com/Lissy93/web-check)|A powerful website analysis tool that can analyze the website's IP information, SSL chain, DNS records, cookies, domain name information, server location and website performance, etc. It is completely open source, free and can be used directly online.
[wewe-rss](https://github.com/cooderl/wewe-rss)|A WeChat public account subscription tool based on WeChat Reading, which realizes public account subscription RSS generation, supports automatic scheduled updates and full-text content output, and supports one-click privatization deployment of Docker, Railway, HuggingFace, etc.
[Applite](https://github.com/milanvarady/Applite)|A Homebrew visual tool for non-technical people. It provides a simple and easy-to-use interface to easily install, update and uninstall applications. It has a carefully selected high-quality application list and can be seamlessly integrated with the existing brew installation environment.
[memos](https://github.com/usememos/memos)|An open source and free lightweight note-taking service that can easily record ideas, knowledge and notes and support public sharing. The deployment is simple and can be completed in a few seconds through Docker and all data can be saved to itself.
[deskflow](https://github.com/deskflow/deskflow)|A cross-device keyboard and mouse sharing utility that controls multiple Windows, macOS, or Linux computers through one keyboard and mouse. It supports clipboard sharing, enables TLS encryption by default, and provides an out-of-the-box installation package.
[iptv-checker](https://github.com/zhimin-dev/iptv-checker)|A tool for checking IPTV available sources. It can detect the iptv-org public channel list and custom sources. It supports Chinese, online viewing and scheduled checking tasks. It is suitable for macOS, Windows and Docker installation and use.
[caesium-image-compressor](https://github.com/Lymphatus/caesium-image-compressor)|A GitHub open source free image compression tool that can effectively reduce file size while maintaining image quality. It supports multiple image formats such as JPG, PNG and WebP, and provides online experience and client downloads.
[TinyPNG4Mac](https://github.com/kyleduo/TinyPNG4Mac)|An open source and free image lossless compression client, which can complete image compression through simple drag and drop operations. Based on TinyPNG, it can achieve almost lossless compression service. The interface is simple and intuitive and supports light and dark theme switching.
[stretchly](https://github.com/hovancik/stretchly)|An open source free rest reminder tool that can flexibly arrange work and rest time reminders, supports setting different reminder sound effects and themes, allows temporary postponement and skipping of breaks, and can be set to display in full screen or transparent mode.
[FileCodeBox](https://github.com/vastsa/FileCodeBox)|An open source tool for sharing text and files with anonymous passwords. It supports copy-paste and drag-and-drop uploading. You can customize the number of shares and the validity period. It can be used without registration and login. It also provides a management panel to view and delete files.
[TranslucentTB](https://github.com/TranslucentTB/TranslucentTB)|An open source and free Windows taskbar beautification tool that supports taskbar transparency and multiple status settings. It is suitable for Windows 10 and Windows 11 systems. It is lightweight and only takes up a few MB of memory.
[PDF-Guru](https://github.com/kevin2li/PDF-Guru)|A multi-functional PDF document processing tool that can remove watermarks in batches, add bookmarks, automatically identify import and OCR, and also supports Anki card making and video note functions, realizing a one-stop solution to multiple needs.
[city-roads](https://github.com/anvaka/city-roads)|An urban road visualization tool that inputs a city name to render the city's main roads into a black and white road map. The data comes from OpenStreetMap, which can quickly obtain road information.
[transok-wails](https://github.com/bent2685/transok-wails)|An open source tool for LAN file sharing, which realizes file sharing through a unique password. It supports drag-and-drop upload and one-click download. It does not limit the number and size of files. It is compatible with Windows and macOS systems. It also has plain text sharing, dark theme and multi-language switching functions.
[wapy.dev](https://github.com/meceware/wapy.dev)|A paid subscription management tool on GitHub that can track and manage regular paid subscription content and remind you to renew through email or push notification when it expires. It supports category classification, custom colors, filtering and one-click Docker deployment.
[superfile](https://github.com/yorukot/superfile)|A high-looking terminal file management tool that provides a simple interface to support common file management operations. It provides 806 available proxies in 60 countries. It can be installed and called in the code through npm.
[faved](https://github.com/denho/faved)|An open source and self-hosted bookmark management tool that provides a simple web interface and a colorful nested tag system. It supports browser gadgets to quickly save links, Pocket data migration, data local storage and one-click deployment through Docker.
[MacCalendar](https://github.com/bylinxx/MacCalendar)|A localized calendar tool designed specifically for macOS users. It is directly integrated into the menu bar, does not occupy desktop space and is available at any time. It supports Chinese lunar calendar display, 24 solar terms markings and most Gregorian calendar lunar festival information. It can read and display system schedule data. It is completely free and open source.
[react-glass-keep](https://github.com/nikunjsingh93/react-glass-keep)|An open source note-taking application similar to Google Keep, providing a simple and beautiful interface, supporting Markdown format, to-do list, real-time collaboration, multi-user management and offline use functions. It can be deployed with one click through Docker or installed with local npm. It also supports PWA installation and data migration from Google Keep.
[Text-Grab](https://github.com/TheJoeFin/Text-Grab)|An open source free text recognition tool based on Windows' built-in OCR API. It can identify any visible text on the screen and copy it directly to the clipboard. It supports multiple working modes such as full-screen interception, frame selection recognition, text editing, quick search, batch processing of image text and command line startup.
[mole](https://github.com/tw93/mole)|An open source free cleaning tool for Mac. It can dig deeply and clean hidden caches, logs, temporary files and completely uninstall applications with one click. It has a built-in protection mechanism to avoid accidentally deleting key system files and supports previewing deleted content.
[Peersuite](https://github.com/openconstruct/Peersuite)|A decentralized open source collaboration platform that provides a private team workspace, supports channel chat and private message functions, and implements end-to-end encrypted communication based on WebRTC. All data is only transmitted on the client and does not pass through the server to ensure privacy and security and avoid service dependence.
[widgets](https://github.com/widget-js/widgets)|A Windows open source desktop component framework. It provides rich widgets such as clock, weather, and system monitoring, supports modular on-demand installation and custom development, and is built on the Vue technology stack. It can be downloaded and used through the Microsoft Store or the official website with one click.
[log-lottery](https://github.com/LOG1997/log-lottery)|A 3D dynamic lottery application specially designed for the annual meeting scene. It supports Excel to import the list of people, customized prizes and backgrounds, default winners and result export. It uses Vue 3+Three.js to build the core 3D rotating sphere interface. It runs purely on the front end and supports automatic recovery of progress after power outage or refresh.
[ScrollSnap](https://github.com/Brkgng/ScrollSnap)|A long screenshot tool for macOS. After selecting the area and scrolling the content naturally, the App will use intelligent algorithms to automatically identify and stitch together into a long picture. The whole process is processed locally to ensure privacy and security. After the screenshot is finished, the picture can be previewed and supports multiple saving locations.
[clip-save](https://github.com/snsogbl/clip-save)|A clipboard management tool used to solve the problem of copied content overwriting. It can automatically save all copied content and support search recovery. It can intelligently identify text, pictures, links and other types and provide targeted functions such as QR code extraction and URL parsing. It is developed as a cross-platform desktop application using the Wails+Vue3 technology stack. The data is stored in a local SQLite database and has practical functions such as password protection.
[budgie-desktop](https://github.com/BuddiesOfBudgie/budgie-desktop)|A lightweight and clean Linux desktop environment that provides a simple and beautiful interface and complete functions. It is built on the GNOME technology stack, adopts a Windows-like classic layout, integrates Raven sidebars and customizable panels, has low system resource usage and is easy to use.
[Beaver-Notes](https://github.com/Beaver-Notes/Beaver-Notes)|A local note-taking application that focuses on privacy and security. All data is stored on the local device and supports encryption of important notes. It provides Markdown writing, note linking, and tag classification functions. It supports multiple cloud synchronization methods and full-platform installation. It is compatible with macOS, Windows, Linux, Android, and iOS systems.
[crossdesk](https://github.com/kunkundi/crossdesk)|An open source remote desktop software that supports web access. You can control remote devices through a browser. It has real-time audio and video transmission, network transparent transmission, codec and transmission encryption capabilities. It supports multiple systems and Docker self-hosting, has low resource usage and fast running speed.
[tiny-svg](https://github.com/hehehai/tiny-svg)|An online tool that integrates SVG optimization and code generation functions. It provides more than 40 optimization options, real-time preview and one-click generation of framework codes such as React/Vue. It supports image editing, multi-format export and PWA offline use.
[crosspaste-desktop](https://github.com/CrossPaste/crosspaste-desktop)|A cross-platform clipboard synchronization open source tool that supports free copying and pasting of text, images, files and other content between multiple devices. It provides a simple interface and intelligent data management. It uses end-to-end encryption and local data storage to protect privacy. It is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems.
[gosuki](https://github.com/blob42/gosuki)|An open source tool for browser bookmark management that can track and synchronize bookmark changes in multiple browsers in real time. All tags are stored in a local database and automatically detect installed browsers. It supports mainstream browsers such as Chrome and Firefox. It has a built-in visual interface and command line tools. Bookmarks can be tagged for quick search and retrieval. It can be installed through Go or compiled from source code. It is compatible with Linux and macOS systems.
[alt-sendme](https://github.com/tonyantony300/alt-sendme)|An open source tool for large file transfer. It supports point-to-point direct transfer of files of any size and format without the need to register an account. It uses end-to-end encryption to ensure security, has a breakpoint resume function, provides an out-of-the-box installation package and is compatible with Windows, Linux and macOS systems.
[stump](https://github.com/stumpapp/stump)|An open source server for managing digital books. It can centrally manage scattered comics and e-books. It provides a simple web interface and OPDS protocol to support cross-device reading. It supports multi-format, multi-account and permission management. It occupies low resources and recommends Docker deployment.
[librepods](https://github.com/kavishdevar/librepods)|An open source tool that breaks through Apple's ecological blockade, allowing non-Apple devices to use all AirPods functions, including noise reduction switching, in-ear detection, etc. Android requires Root permissions and the Xposed framework. The Linux version is under development and is compatible with the second/third generation AirPods Pro.
[xleak](https://github.com/bgreenwell/xleak)|An Excel file processing tool for servers or remote terminals. It can view and operate Excel files directly on the terminal without installing Microsoft Excel. It supports cell navigation, full-text search, formula viewing, data copying and multiple format exports, and is compatible with multiple systems and table formats.
[PicView](https://github.com/Ruben2776/PicView)|A cross-platform image viewing tool that supports dozens of formats such as JPG, PNG, PSD, RAW, HEIC, SVG, dynamic WEBP, and GIF. It has image browsing, cropping, batch conversion, EXIF ​​editing, and compression functions. It provides an out-of-the-box installation package and is compatible with Windows and macOS systems.
[ticker](https://github.com/achannarasappa/ticker)|A stock and cryptocurrency monitoring tool for terminal environments, which can track price fluctuations and position portfolio returns in real time, and supports multi-batch purchase records and accurate average cost calculations.
[MacsyZones](https://github.com/rohanrhu/MacsyZones)|A desktop window layout management tool for macOS systems that supports custom creation of screen layout areas. When dragging the window, it will automatically snap to a preset position. Multiple layout plans can be saved and quickly switched. It is compatible with independent layout configurations of multiple monitors.
[mcat](https://github.com/Skardyy/mcat)|A terminal file processing tool that supports previewing PDF, Word, PPT, pictures and playing videos in the terminal. It has the ability to merge and convert multiple files into Markdown, HTML or pictures. It provides multiple installation methods such as Homebrew and Winget, and is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems.
[CloudPaste](https://github.com/ling-drag0n/CloudPaste)|An online clipboard and file sharing tool based on Cloudflare. It supports Markdown editing, real-time preview and various S3 storage server file uploads. It provides sharing rules such as access passwords, expiration time and view limit, supports WebDAV protocol mounting as a network drive, and has GitHub Actions automatic deployment and Docker one-click deployment and other rapid construction methods.
[qView](https://github.com/jurplel/qView)|A minimalist and easy-to-use open source image viewing tool, it has functions such as loading Gif animations, file history, image rotation/mirror, multi-thread preloading, etc. It supports all common image formats, can be started in almost seconds without waiting and does not occupy resources. It is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems.
[ghostty-config](https://github.com/zerebos/ghostty-config)|A visual configuration generator for Ghostty terminal that supports adjusting fonts, colors, cursor styles and window transparency in the web interface, provides real-time preview function, can export configuration files with one click and supports local deployment.
[Enterr](https://github.com/JonBunator/Enterr)|An open source automation tool for managing website accounts. It manages accounts through a simple web interface and automatically logs in regularly to keep them active. It simulates real user operations and supports command lines to complete management functions such as user creation, deletion and password modification.
[cad-viewer](https://github.com/mlightcad/cad-viewer)|An open source CAD drawing viewing tool that runs in the browser. It does not require a back-end server to ensure file privacy. It supports operations such as smooth rendering of DWG/DXF files, layer management, and entity selection. It is based on the Tree.js engine and uses GPU acceleration to maintain high frame rates. It can be integrated into mainstream frameworks and platforms.
[bentopdf](https://github.com/alam00000/bentopdf)|An open source PDF toolbox for local processing, providing more than 50 practical tools such as merging and splitting, editing and conversion, and OCR recognition. It supports multiple file format conversions and damaged PDF repairs. It can be deployed with one click through Docker or downloaded files to a web server.
[reconya](https://github.com/Dyneteq/reconya)|An open source tool for LAN asset discovery and monitoring. It can automatically scan and identify network devices and deeply analyze MAC addresses, manufacturers, host names and operating systems. It is built based on Go and HTMX and has a built-in visual web dashboard. It supports IPv4 active scanning and IPv6 passive monitoring.
[xl-converter](https://github.com/JacobDev1/xl-converter)|An image format conversion and processing tool that supports mutual conversion and parallel processing of JPEG XL, AVIF and other formats. It provides lossless JPEG transcoding (16%-22% reduction and reversible) and resizing according to resolution. It is compatible with Windows and Linux systems.
[gcopy](https://github.com/llaoj/gcopy)|An open source web tool that solves the problem of cross-device clipboard synchronization. It supports text, screenshots and file transfers without the need to install a client. The data will automatically expire after 24 hours in the memory and supports end-to-end encrypted transmission.
[aio-usb-drive](https://github.com/fathulfahmy/aio-usb-drive)|A tool for creating an all-round system maintenance USB flash drive. Various ISO image files can be dragged directly into the USB flash drive to enable multiple systems and tools to be started at the same time. Common tools such as SystemRescue rescue toolkit and Rescuezilla disk cloning are selected and detailed tutorials are provided. 32GB can hold most tools.
[fk-desktop](https://github.com/flowkeeper-org/fk-desktop)|An open source Pomodoro desktop application specially built for efficiency control. Its core function is to focus on work and time recording. The interface is simple and interference-free. It supports offline use on all platforms and local data storage.
[pdf-document-layout-analysis](https://github.com/huridocs/pdf-document-layout-analysis)|An open source project focusing on complex document layout analysis, it can accurately identify more than 11 element types such as titles and tables and determine the reading order. It supports PDF conversion to Markdown/HTML, OCR recognition of more than 150 languages ​​and multi-language translation. It provides Web UI and REST API, and supports one-click deployment of Docker.
[PasteMD](https://github.com/RICHQAQ/PasteMD)|An open source tool that solves the problem of importing Markdown content into Office document format. Read the Markdown of the clipboard or the HTML of the web page, call the Pandoc engine to transcode and automatically insert the cursor position. It supports intelligent recognition of Markdown tables and one-click pasting into Excel while retaining the format. It provides a portable version and an integrated version containing Pandoc, without the need for cumbersome configuration of the environment.
[StartupFolder](https://github.com/FuzzyIdeas/StartupFolder)|A startup item management tool for macOS that enables applications, scripts and other projects to start automatically at startup through a dedicated folder in the user directory. It provides a forced hidden startup mode to solve pop-up window problems, and has an automatic restart and keep-alive function after the program unexpectedly crashes.
[readur](https://github.com/readur/readur)|An open source document management system that supports unified management of multi-format documents such as PDF, pictures, and Office. It extracts text through OCR to achieve full-text search. It can be connected to WebDAV, local folders or S3 storage and automatically synchronized. It provides tag classification, folder monitoring and other functions, and supports Docker one-click deployment.
[ueli](https://github.com/oliverschwendener/ueli)|A minimalist cross-platform keyboard launcher, it supports opening applications instantly after being called up through Alt+Space, searching for files and performing mathematical calculations, currency and color conversions. It provides a rich plug-in ecosystem that can call VS Code projects, browser bookmarks, etc. It requires no mouse operation and is completely open source. It is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems.
[cross-seed](https://github.com/cross-seed/cross-seed)|An open source auxiliary seeding tool for PT players. It automatically scans local resources and matches the same seeds in multiple sites. You can add seeding tasks without re-downloading. It supports mainstream download clients, accurately identifies renamed or sorted files through IMDb/TMDB ID, and can cooperate with autobrr to achieve second-level auxiliary seeding and Docker container deployment.
[raweb](https://github.com/kimmknight/raweb)|An open source tool for RemoteApp management for Windows 10/11 systems. It provides a web interface in the Fluent 2 design language, supports the addition, editing and removal of remote applications, and synchronizes applications to the local start menu or mobile phone remote desktop app through the Workspace subscription function. The deployment process provides a one-click PowerShell script to automatically handle IIS and component installation.
[domainstack.io](https://github.com/jakejarvis/domainstack.io)|An open source domain name analysis tool. By entering a domain name, you can obtain key information such as WHOIS, DNS records, SSL certificates, SEO data, etc. in one stop. It can also extract meta tags, generate website screenshots, and adopts a multi-layer intelligent caching mechanism. No registration is required and the response speed is fast.
[fzfm](https://github.com/ashish0kumar/fzfm)|A terminal file manager built on fzf, which quickly locates files through fuzzy search. It provides a simple interactive interface to support operations such as browsing, previewing, copying, and moving. It is compatible with Linux and macOS systems and supports real-time preview of multiple types of files and batch processing.
[WhiteSur-gtk-theme](https://github.com/vinceliuice/WhiteSur-gtk-theme)|A Linux GTK theme that replicates the macOS design style. Deeply customize GTK 3/4 applications, GNOME Shell and GDM login interfaces, restore blur effects and rounded corner designs, provide one-click installation of automated scripts, support adjustment of accent colors, transparency and sidebar styles, and uniformly take over Firefox and Flatpak application styles.
[twake-drive](https://github.com/linagora/twake-drive)|An open source alternative cloud storage solution to Google Drive, which supports file upload, sharing collaboration, rights management and online document editing. It integrates OnlyOffice and encrypts stored data. It can be controlled through one-click deployment with Docker or private server installation.
[SlideSCI](https://github.com/Achuan-2/SlideSCI)|An open source plug-in specially designed for scientific research PPT production. It solves the pain points of adding picture titles, batch alignment, and position copying. It supports direct insertion of formatted Markdown content, code block highlighting, and LaTeX formulas. It is compatible with Windows-side PowerPoint and some WPS functions.
[Wallos](https://github.com/ellite/Wallos)|A self-hosted tool for managing personal subscription services. It can aggregate decentralized bills, provide a dashboard overview, send expiration reminders, support multi-currency conversion and file upload management, and uses Docker for one-click deployment and local data storage.

### Useful Plugins

Project|Description
---|---
