[bypass-paywalls-firefox-clean](https://github.com/bpc-clone/bypass-paywalls-firefox-clean)|A browser plug-in for bypassing website paywalls. It can help users read articles that originally require payment for free. It supports Chrome and Firefox browsers and is mainly suitable for information websites.
[bookmarks-artist](https://github.com/liujuntao123/bookmarks-artist)|A high-looking browser bookmark viewing tool that uses a grid layout to divide folders, making the folder hierarchy clearer and clear at a glance. It is not currently available in the Chrome extension store, but you can download the compressed package from GitHub.
[mp-vx-insight](https://github.com/pudongping/mp-vx-insight)|A Chrome extension specially designed for WeChat public accounts, which can automatically capture article titles, authors, introductions, and cover images, extract article content, and copy historical article addresses with one click, improving the efficiency of public account content creation and management.
[code-box](https://github.com/027xiguapi/code-box)|A browser plug-in suitable for mainstream technology community websites, it can copy code with one click, read the full text, remove login pop-ups without logging in, and download articles as html or markdown files with one click. [Scholar-PDF-Reader-with-Annotations](https://github.com/salcc/Scholar-PDF-Reader-with-Annotations)|An open source extension for Google Scholar PDF Reader that supports text highlighting in multiple colors, management annotations, and JSON import and export annotations, while retaining the original reference preview and AI outline functions.
[Overleaf-Bib-Helper](https://github.com/Arvid-pku/Overleaf-Bib-Helper)|An open source plug-in for Overleaf that can search DBLP and Google Scholar academic documents directly in the Overleaf interface, obtain and insert standard format BibTeX citation information with one click, and supports precise search, automatic processing of special characters, and batch import functions.
[quick-prompt](https://github.com/wenyuanw/quick-prompt)|An open source and free browser plug-in, focusing on prompt word management and fast input. Allows you to quickly insert preset prompt words into any web page input box, supports shortcut keys and right-click menus to save selected text as prompt words, provides complete prompt word management functions, including titles, content and tags, supports export, import and search filtering, and automatically adapts to the system light and dark themes.
[sync-your-cookie](https://github.com/jackluson/sync-your-cookie)|A Chrome extension for crawler cookie management. It synchronizes the real login status of the browser to the crawler script through Cloudflare cloud. It supports multi-account rotation and visual management. It uses protobuf coding to ensure transmission security. It can also automatically merge push rules and achieve data synchronization between development and production environments.
[hoverzoom](https://github.com/extesy/hoverzoom)|An open source and free browser plug-in that helps users enlarge pictures in high definition when browsing the web. Just hover the mouse over the picture to view the high-definition large picture. It supports custom shortcut key operations, adapts to hundreds of popular websites and allows adding or disabling related functions.
[cpdown](https://github.com/ysm-dev/cpdown)|A browser plug-in for web content processing, which can copy any web content with one click and convert the copied content into Markdown format, solving the problem of directly copying messy HTML tags.
[beachpatrol](https://github.com/sebastiancarlos/beachpatrol)|A browser automation CLI tool built on Playwright, which uses command line scripts to control the browser to perform repetitive tasks such as daily check-in and checking emails. It supports Chromium and Firefox browsers. It has built-in anti-detection technology to avoid automated identification mechanisms. It provides supporting browser extensions and client/server architecture, and can be integrated into other scripts.
[EasyCopy](https://github.com/joeseesun/EasyCopy)|An open source and free Chrome plug-in that quickly copies web content through different click operations. Click to copy the title and URL of the current web page (one line each), supporting convenient web page information extraction.
[favbox](https://github.com/dd3v/favbox)|An enhanced browser bookmark management plug-in that converts text list tags into intuitive card displays, automatically scans and extracts web page titles, descriptions, and thumbnails, and supports advanced search filtering by domain names, folders, and keywords. It can detect duplicate and invalid bookmarks, and has a local note-taking function.
[read-frog](https://github.com/mengxi-ream/read-frog)|An open source browser translation plug-in that focuses on deep language learning. It supports immersive translation. It can provide detailed explanations of words, phrases and sentences in articles and automatically extract core content, achieving a seamless combination of reading and learning.
[biliplus](https://github.com/0xlau/biliplus)|A browser plug-in used to optimize the browsing experience of Bilibili. Provides a homepage clean mode to hide hot searches and redundant elements, and supports practical functions such as obtaining video covers, AI summaries, infinite speed adjustment, and blocking comments from specified users.
[scriptcat](https://github.com/scriptscat/scriptcat)|A powerful browser script plug-in, compatible with Oil Monkey script, supports background running and scheduled tasks, and can continue to run even if the web page is closed. Combined with cloud synchronization and script subscription functions, script management can be easily realized.
[FluentRead](https://github.com/Bistutu/FluentRead)|An open source browser translation plug-in designed to provide a native-like immersive reading experience. It supports practical functions such as bilingual comparison, word translation, and full-text translation. It provides rich customization options and shortcut key operations, and is compatible with more than 20 translation service providers.
[cookie-editor](https://github.com/Moustachauve/cookie-editor)|An open source extension for browser cookie management, providing a simple and intuitive visual interface, supporting the rapid creation, editing, deletion and batch clearing of cookies on the current page. It is compatible with mainstream browsers and mobile terminals.
[cloud-document-converter](https://github.com/whale4113/cloud-document-converter)|A browser plug-in for converting Feishu document formats. It supports downloading or copying Feishu documents into standard Markdown files that retain elements such as titles, lists, tables, pictures, etc. It is compatible with browsers such as Chrome, Edge, and Firefox.
[markdown-viewer-extension](https://github.com/xicilion/markdown-viewer-extension)|An open source browser plug-in for Markdown document processing, which supports previewing Markdown in the browser and exporting well-formatted Word documents with one click. It can convert Mermaid flow charts into high-definition images and LaTeX mathematical formulas into Word editable formula objects. The code can be automatically highlighted and supports 100+ programming languages ​​and 18+ professional themes.
[internet_archive_downloader](https://github.com/elementdavv/internet_archive_downloader)|A browser plug-in for downloading time-limited borrowed books from Internet Archive or HathiTrust. It can download book content as a PDF with text embedded or a JPEG/PNG picture set plus text file. It supports simultaneous downloading of multiple books, quality selection and page range settings. It obtains content page by page and builds a file stream in real time to write to disk. It has low memory usage and is compatible with mainstream browsers.

[res-downloader](https://github.com/putyy/res-downloader)|A network resource sniffing and downloading tool on GitHub that supports video, pictures, audio, m3u8, live streaming and special network resources. [Hitomi-Downloader](https://github.com/KurtBestor/Hitomi-Downloader)|A desktop download tool on GitHub that can download videos, images, music, text and other content from major websites. [wx_channels_download](https://github.com/ltaoo/wx_channels_download)|An open source video account download tool that supports one-click downloading of video pages, multi-definition selection and live broadcast playback downloads. [pilipala](https://github.com/guozhigq/pilipala)|A third-party Bilibili client that provides video recommendations, drama lists, popular live broadcasts, user login, video playback and interactive functions, and is suitable for Android and iOS devices.
[plyr](https://github.com/sampotts/plyr)|A lightweight front-end video player that supports playing videos on mainstream platforms such as YouTube and Vimeo. [melody](https://github.com/foamzou/melody)|An open source music management tool that solves the problem of multi-platform music playback restrictions. It supports searching and extracting music from 9 major music platforms, provides audition and quality selection, and can download locally and upload to cloud disk with one click for resource management and playback.
[netease-cloud-music](https://github.com/chaunsin/netease-cloud-music)|A Golang API interface toolkit for NetEase Cloud Music, which implements functions such as automatic check-in, music partner evaluation, browsing 300 songs, music downloads, etc. It greatly simplifies the daily music task operation process and provides a complete Golang API interface and command line tools.
[bili-shadowreplay](https://github.com/Xinrea/bili-shadowreplay)|A tool focused on live broadcast processing at Bilibili. It can cache live broadcasts and perform real-time editing and submission. It compresses the slicing and submission process to the minute level by delimiting time intervals and supports adding multiple live broadcast rooms for management.
[nuclear](https://github.com/nukeop/nuclear)|A desktop music player focusing on free streaming resources, it pulls free music resources from the Internet and provides a beautiful and powerful playback interface. It supports YouTube music search and playback, album search, automatic song query and song queue functions based on artist and track name.
[FreeTube](https://github.com/FreeTubeApp/FreeTube)|A GitHub open source third-party desktop client for YouTube that supports viewing search and subscription channels, playback history, saved videos, multiple themes, and mini playback windows. It enables ad-free viewing and prevents Google from using cookies and JavaScript tracking.
[youtube-music](https://github.com/th-ch/youtube-music)|A third-party YouTube music client built on Electron, retaining the native interface and experience, providing rich plug-in support functions and appearance customization, and a built-in ad blocker that can automatically block ads and tracking.
[AlgerMusicPlayer](https://github.com/algerkong/AlgerMusicPlayer)|A full-platform music player that supports NetEase Cloud account login synchronization and gray music resource analysis, provides immersive lyrics display and rich customization options, can download music files without relying on online APIs, and has an automatic update check function.
[StreamCap](https://github.com/ihmily/StreamCap)|A client for live stream recording that supports 40+ domestic and foreign mainstream live broadcast platforms. It has the core functions of cyclically monitoring the status of the live broadcast room immediately after recording and checking scheduled tasks. It is an open source and free tool on GitHub.
[zay-cloud](https://github.com/fivif/zay-cloud)|A local cloud media server that provides a simple and intuitive interface, supports preview playback of multiple file types and WebDAV network access, can manage files, media and documents, and has various video format playback and sliding switching interface functions similar to Douyin.
[Kazumi](https://github.com/Predidit/Kazumi)|A cross-platform open source application for collecting and watching TV dramas online on GitHub. It supports custom rule import and sharing, streaming online viewing, video barrage, real-time super-resolution, multiple video sources and wireless screen projection. It provides basic functions such as drama catalog, search, and schedule, and is compatible with the Android platform.
[telegram-files](https://github.com/jarvis2f/telegram-files)|A self-hosted Telegram file download tool that supports batch downloading of files from channels and groups. It has the ability to work together with multiple accounts, enables continuous and stable unattended downloading, and supports pausing, resuming downloads and automatically transferring files.
[streamyfin](https://github.com/streamyfin/streamyfin)|A Jellyfin mobile client application built on Expo. It provides the functions of skipping the beginning/end of the film, video preview thumbnail, background audio playback, experimental media download and screencasting, and media request. It has been put on the App Store and supports Android users to download APK.
[youtube-transcripts-machine](https://github.com/zaidmukaddam/youtube-transcripts-machine)|A YouTube video processing tool on GitHub. Just paste the video URL to get the complete transcript and interactive timestamp. It supports one-click copy and download functions.
[Netease_url](https://github.com/Suxiaoqinx/Netease_url)|An open source tool for lossless analysis of NetEase Cloud music on GitHub. It can analyze a variety of sound qualities by copying and pasting song links, including ultra-clear master tapes and immersive surround sound. It supports packaged downloading of song files, cover images and lyrics.
[Parabolic](https://github.com/NickvisionApps/Parabolic)|A video download tool based on yt-dlp, which supports downloading videos from thousands of websites and converting them into multiple formats. The interface is simple and friendly, making the video downloading process easy and convenient.
[biliLive-tools](https://github.com/renmu123/biliLive-tools)|A one-stop processing tool for recording and broadcasting at Bilibili. It supports automated webhook processing for Biliji, blrec, and DDTV. It can realize barrage conversion, video suppression, and uploading at Bilibili, solving the problem of fragmentation of recording and broadcasting tools.
[music-website](https://github.com/Yin-Hongwei/music-website)|A music website project with separate front-end and back-end, supporting music playback and lyrics synchronization functions. The client and management side use the Vue3 framework, and the server side is implemented using Spring Boot + MyBatis.
[docker-xiaoya](https://github.com/monlor/docker-xiaoya)|An open source tool for online playback of network disk resources. One-click deployment of Alist+Emby service through Docker Compose to achieve convenient access and playback. It supports mainstream network disks, automatically updates cloud disk data and media information, is compatible with the X86 platform, and is simple to operate without complex configuration.
[bitplay](https://github.com/aculix/bitplay)|A Torrent video streaming web application that supports playing magnet links or torrent files directly in the browser without downloading in full. Integrate Prowlar and Jackett search functions, provide SOCKS5 proxy to protect privacy, support conversion of SRT subtitles to VTT format, and can be deployed through Docker or Go.
[OpenUtau](https://github.com/stakira/OpenUtau)|An open source tool for music creation and editing, it provides a simple and intuitive operation interface, is compatible with UTAU music library and sampler, supports shortcut keys, VSQX format import, multi-language and pre-rendering functions, and can easily create high-quality singing synthesis works.
[LibreTV](https://github.com/bestZwei/LibreTV)|A lightweight online video search and viewing platform that can be used without registration. It integrates multiple reliable video sources and provides search and playback functions. It has built-in ad filtering and adopts responsive design to adapt to multiple devices.
[BiliHistoryFrontend](https://github.com/2977094657/BiliHistoryFrontend)|A tool for historical record analysis of Bilibili. It can comprehensively analyze viewing habits and generate detailed data reports, including statistics of viewing duration, generation of annual summary, analysis of title keywords and viewing completion rate. It also supports local video summary generation and one-click download of all videos. It is developed based on Vue 3 and deployed through Docker with one click, providing a visual operation interface.
[MonsterMusic](https://github.com/ZTFtrue/MonsterMusic)|A privacy-focused Android local music player, it provides professional-grade equalizer to adjust sound effects, supports multiple theme switching and lyrics display, and special functions can be triggered by clicking on the lyrics.
[m3u8player](https://github.com/ronething/m3u8player)|An open source, ultra-simple streaming media player that supports playing various HLS format video streams, perfectly solves the problem of cross-domain restrictions, and provides a local proxy function to forward file requests. It supports multiple CORS processing modes, dark themes, and drag-and-drop file uploads. It can be deployed and used with one line of commands.
[webcamize](https://github.com/cowtoolz/webcamize)|An open source tool that can improve camera image quality. It can convert any camera into a high-quality webcam. By coordinating the gphoto2 and ffmpeg tools, inserting the camera running command can output the video to a virtual video device. The operation is simple and suitable for technical novices.
[HandBrake](https://github.com/HandBrake/HandBrake)|An open source video transcoding tool that supports converting formats from DVD, Blu-ray and various video files. It provides device optimization presets and intelligent encoding algorithms, which can compress file size and maintain image quality. It has functions such as subtitle adjustment and batch processing, and is compatible with Windows, Linux and macOS systems.
[ScreenToGif](https://github.com/NickeManarin/ScreenToGif)|An open source and free Windows screen recording tool that can flexibly record any area of ​​the screen, camera and built-in drawing board. It provides a simple and easy-to-use interface and built-in editor. It supports cropping, adding text and special effects, and can be saved as GIF, video and other formats.
[FieldStation42](https://github.com/shane-mason/FieldStation42)|A nostalgic radio and television simulation open source project for the post-8090 generation, it can perfectly simulate the traditional cable and broadcast television viewing experience, automatically play corresponding programs according to the current time, support seamless switching of multiple channels, automatically generate weekly program schedules, and intelligently insert advertisements and channel logos.
[QLVideo](https://github.com/Marginal/QLVideo)|An open source tool for enhanced preview capabilities for Apple computers. It displays thumbnails and previews of videos in multiple formats in Finder, and adds support for non-native video formats such as .flv, .mkv, and .webm.
[LibreTube](https://github.com/libre-tube/LibreTube)|A high-looking YouTube open source alternative client that supports subscribing to channels, creating subscription groups and custom playlists. It adopts Material Design 3 design style, provides Piped API and local modes to obtain content stably and smoothly and automatically skip ads.
[Cap](https://github.com/CapSoftware/Cap)|A cross-platform video recording tool that can be used as an open source alternative to Loom. It supports desktop clients and web versions to quickly complete video recording, editing and sharing, and provides data self-hosting functions to ensure users have complete control over their data.
[downkyicore](https://github.com/yaobiao131/downkyicore)|A cross-platform Bilibili video downloading open source tool. It supports batch downloading of 8K, HDR, and Dolby Vision high-definition videos, and provides practical functions such as audio and video extraction and watermark removal. Developed based on AvaloniaUI, it is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems and provides out-of-the-box installation packages.
[OpenCut](https://github.com/OpenCut-app/OpenCut)|CapCut is an open source alternative cross-platform video editor that supports timeline editing, multi-track, and real-time preview functions. Videos are saved locally without watermarks and no subscription is required. It is compatible with web, desktop, and mobile terminals.
[xiaomusic](https://github.com/hanxi/xiaomusic)|An open source Xiaoai speaker enhancement tool that automatically downloads music through yt-dlp and supports voice-controlled playback. It can play niche and local music files, provides favorites management, Web console configuration and multi-device compatibility, and supports Docker one-click deployment.
[Y2A-Auto](https://github.com/fqscfqj/Y2A-Auto)|An automated tool for cross-platform video transfer. It can transfer high-quality YouTube videos to AcFun with one click. It provides a complete solution from video download and subtitle translation to automatic upload and content review. It also supports automatic monitoring of YouTube trending videos and designated channels.
[StreamCap](https://github.com/ihmily/StreamCap)|A cross-platform live broadcast automation recording tool that enables start-up monitoring and batch recording of 40+ domestic and foreign mainstream live broadcast platforms. It supports loop detection of live broadcast status, scheduled task settings, multiple output formats and automatic transcoding to mp4 format after recording. It also provides message push function, covering Douyin, Bilibili, TikTok, Twitch and other platforms.
[openspot-music-app](https://github.com/BlackHatDevX/openspot-music-app)|An open source and free cross-platform music player that supports high-quality lossless sound quality for online playback and offline downloading. It can be used without registration or login. It is compatible with mobile and computer terminals and has no ads.
[wiliwili](https://github.com/xfangfang/wiliwili)|A third-party Bilibili client specially designed for controller users. It is suitable for Switch, PS4 and other game devices, provides a browsing experience close to the official PC client, and supports multiple control methods such as touch screen, mouse, keyboard and controller.
[MoonTV](https://github.com/senshinya/MoonTV)|A cross-platform film and television aggregation player with dozens of built-in free resource sites, supports one-time search for film and television resources on the entire network, and has intelligent ad removal and smooth online playback functions.
[BiliTools](https://github.com/btjawa/BiliTools)|An all-in-one resource download tool specially created for Bilibili. It supports 4K, 8K, HDR, Dolby Vision and other high-definition video downloads, and can obtain complete content such as barrages, subtitles, covers and AI summaries.
[dl-librescore](https://github.com/LibreScore/dl-librescore)|An open source tool for downloading MuseScore music scores. It supports MIDI, MP3, PDF and other format downloads and PNG, SVG, WAV format conversion. It has full platform compatibility and batch processing capabilities. It is completely free and the code is open source.
[f2](https://github.com/Johnserf-Seed/f2)|A Python tool library for batch downloading of network resources. It supports the downloading of videos, pictures, and audios from multiple platforms such as DouYin, TikTok, and Twitter. It also has the functions of live stream recording and barrage collection.
[Petrichor](https://github.com/kushalpandya/Petrichor)|A simple and beautiful offline music player that supports multiple audio formats such as MP3, FLAC, and M4A. It can automatically scan and organize music folders and extract metadata to generate a music library to meet the basic needs of local music management.
[OrionTV](https://github.com/orion-lib/OrionTV)|A video player specially built for TV, supports Apple TV and Android TV, optimizes the UI for TV remote control interaction, is developed using Expo + React Native TVOS, and has file system routing structure and navigation logic.
[YTSage](https://github.com/oop7/YTSage)|An open source YouTube download tool for technical novices. It supports video downloading of any quality, audio extraction, subtitle acquisition and embedding, automatic removal of advertisements, and video cutting by time period. It provides a simple and easy-to-use interface, which can be installed with one click through pip or downloaded the corresponding system executable file.
[sakura](https://github.com/Sarthak2143/sakura)|A multimedia processing library for servers or pure terminal environments. It can render high-quality pictures, GIF animations and videos on the command line. It supports simultaneous playback of audio and video. It uses about 1,000 lines of code to implement functions such as SIXEL graphics rendering, multi-format compatibility and adaptive scaling.
[obs-zoom-to-mouse](https://github.com/BlankSourceCode/obs-zoom-to-mouse)|An open source OBS plug-in for realizing the smart zoom function of the mouse. It supports automatic adjustment of the zoom and position of the display capture source following mouse movement, and provides features such as one-click zooming, customizing zoom multiples and animation speeds, setting following boundaries and locking sensitivity, and is compatible with Windows, Linux, and macOS platforms.
[pigeon-pod](https://github.com/aizhimou/pigeon-pod)|An open source tool that converts YouTube channels into podcast subscriptions. It automatically downloads audio and generates RSS links by providing the channel URL. It supports automatic synchronization updates, keyword filtering, duration filtering and advertisement removal. It provides Docker one-click deployment and is compatible with any podcast client.
[media-downloader](https://github.com/mhogomchungu/media-downloader)|A graphical interface application that integrates multiple video download tools. You can download videos with one click by copying and pasting links and selecting tools. It supports unlimited concurrent batch downloads, multi-language interfaces and is available out-of-the-box on Windows, macOS, and Linux systems.
[ErsatzTV](https://github.com/ErsatzTV/ErsatzTV)|An open source project that converts media libraries into live TV channels. It supports reading local files or connecting to servers such as Plex and Jellyfin. It can customize channels and arrange programs. It can set opening and closing credits and advertising content, and simulate traditional TV experience through IPTV and HDHomeRun.
[OuonnkiTV](https://github.com/Ouonnki/OuonnkiTV)|An open source video search aggregation tool that aggregates and searches multiple video sources to achieve one-stop resource access. It supports real-time search prompts, viewing history and batch import of video sources, and accesses multiple video APIs through a unified interface.
[MuJing](https://github.com/tangshimin/MuJing)|An English learning tool based on real context. It assists word memory through the context in movies, American TV series or documents. It combines interactive methods such as spelling exercises, subtitle transcription and dictation tests. It provides intelligent barrage playback, personalized vocabulary generation and cross-media vocabulary linking functions. It supports Windows and macOS systems.
[rhythm](https://github.com/dexter-xD/rhythm)|A lightweight music player specially built for Linux systems, it supports MP3, FLAC and other audio formats, provides real-time spectrum visualization and particle effects, has CLI and GUI dual interfaces and intelligent playlist management, and the native C core ensures high-performance audio processing.
[xiaomusic](https://github.com/hanxi/xiaomusic)|An open source tool for Xiaoai speakers that supports free playback of local music, search and download of songs across the entire network, and provides voice command control of playback operations. Quick deployment using Docker, compatible with popular Xiaoai speaker models.
[persepolis](https://github.com/persepolisdm/persepolis)|An open source download tool that improves download efficiency and management experience. It supports multi-threaded download acceleration, intelligent task scheduling and download queue management. It can download content from YouTube and other video websites and integrate with browser plug-ins. It is cross-platform compatible with Windows, macOS, and Linux systems.
[synctv](https://github.com/synctv-org/synctv)|An open source tool for synchronized viewing in remote locations. It can ensure the synchronization of operations when multiple people watch videos. It integrates chat and barrage functions, supports analysis of video sources and live broadcast sources such as Alist, Bilibili, Emby, etc., and provides binary files and Docker local deployment methods.
[stremio-web](https://github.com/Stremio/stremio-web)|An open source media center for centralized management of video resources. It integrates various video sources through plug-ins, provides a unified content discovery interface, personal media library management and cross-platform web access. It supports Docker deployment and pnpm local running development server.
[lofi-engine](https://github.com/meel-hd/lofi-engine)|A LoFi music generation desktop application for improving concentration. It can programmatically generate unique audio tracks and customize environmental sound effects, visual themes and music effects. It supports full keyboard shortcut operations and completely local offline operation. It is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems.
[Watcharr](https://github.com/sbondCo/Watcharr)|A self-hosted movie viewing recording tool that can track the viewing status and progress of movies, TV series, animations and games. It supports ratings, list management and content discovery, provides light and dark themes, is quickly deployed through Docker and has complete data control.
[varia](https://github.com/giantpinkrobots/varia)|An open source download manager that integrates multiple types of download functions. It manages file, torrent and video streaming download tasks in a unified manner. It supports audio and video downloads and format conversion from mainstream video websites. It is based on aria2 and yt-dlp to ensure stability and compatibility, and provides browser extensions to enable one-click downloading.
[ytdownloader](https://github.com/aandrew-me/ytdownloader)|An open source and free video download tool that supports downloading videos or audio from mainstream platforms such as YouTube, Facebook, Instagram, TikTok, Twitter, etc., with built-in hardware acceleration and video compression functions.
[pikatorrent](https://github.com/G-Ray/pikatorrent)|An open source and free BT torrent download client that provides a simple and easy-to-use download management interface and supports playing videos while downloading, achieving full platform coverage without payment or platform restrictions.
[openscreen](https://github.com/siddharthvaddem/openscreen)|A simplified free version of Screen Studio's cross-platform screen recording tool that supports recording full screen or specific windows, manually adding custom scaling effects, cropping videos, and setting diverse backgrounds. It has motion blur and smooth transition effects. It is built based on TypeScript. It has no watermarks, no subscription fees, and allows commercial use.
[biu](https://github.com/wood3n/biu)|A cross-platform desktop music player developed based on the API of Bilibili. It provides a simple interface and customized theme styles. It supports searching and playing Bilibili music videos, logging in to obtain favorites information, and giving priority to pulling high-quality audio formats such as lossless Flac and 192K/Hi-Res.
[AutoSubSync](https://github.com/denizsafak/AutoSubSync)|An open source subtitle synchronization tool that automatically analyzes the audio to complete synchronization by dragging and dropping uploaded video and subtitle files. It supports batch processing of the entire series, multiple mainstream subtitle formats, and millisecond-level manual adjustment. It provides cross-platform out-of-the-box executable files.
[BilibiliDown](https://github.com/nICEnnnnnnnLee/BilibiliDown)|An open source Bilibili video download tool that supports downloading of individual videos, favorites, all UP master's works, and watch later lists. It can save subtitles, barrages, interactive videos, and course content, and provides a visual interface to simplify operations.
[seek-tune](https://github.com/cgzirim/seek-tune)|A local song recognition system that provides a simple web interface, generates fingerprint matching songs by analyzing audio features, integrates Spotify and YouTube APIs to obtain information and download links, supports local song library management and two databases, SQLite and MongoDB, and can be deployed with Docker or installed locally.
[8mb.local](https://github.com/JMS1717/8mb.local)|An open source tool for video compression that can accurately compress videos to a specified target size. Supports drag-and-drop operations and graphics card hardware acceleration, displays progress in real time, automatically re-compresses when exceeded, supports concurrent multi-task processing, and local deployment to protect privacy.
[reddix](https://github.com/ck-zhang/reddix)|A terminal client for Reddit users that supports browsing posts, previewing images and playing videos on the terminal. It has multi-account management, keyboard shortcut operation, intelligent caching mechanism and NSFW content filtering functions. It realizes picture display through Kitty protocol and integrates mpv for video playback.
[Magicbrake](https://github.com/danieldelaney/Magicbrake)|An open source video format conversion tool developed based on HandBrake, which supports drag-and-drop upload of video files to quickly convert to MP4 format with one click. No complex configuration is required and an out-of-the-box installation package is provided. Currently, it only supports macOS systems.
[torrra](https://github.com/stabldev/torrra)|An open source download tool in a terminal environment that supports the entire process of seed search and download, provides a simple and intuitive visual interface and functions such as pause and resume. It integrates Jackett/Prowlarr to realize search and ensures stable and efficient downloading based on Libtorrent.
[music-you](https://github.com/GuMengYu/music-you)|An open source desktop music player that returns to the pure experience. It provides NetEase Cloud Music scan code login, private FM, cloud disk and local music playback functions. It supports word-by-word lyrics display and complete music library management.
[zrythm](https://github.com/zrythm/zrythm)|A cross-platform, open source digital audio workstation designed for professionals and beginners alike. It provides a simple interface and smooth operation, with flexible editing of audio materials, piano roll and chord functions, powerful automatic parameter control and support for multiple plug-in formats. It is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems.
[popcorntime](https://github.com/popcorntime/popcorntime)|An open source playback platform that aggregates film and television resources. It updates film and television data every week and supports the use of all platforms. It can aggregate film and TV series resources, covering macOS, Windows, Android, iOS and TV, and can manage local files at the same time.
[Constrict](https://github.com/Wartybix/Constrict)|A local tool for video compression. Input the target file size to automatically calculate the best encoding parameters. It is built on FFmpeg and supports batch processing, custom frame rate limits and multiple encoding format selections.
[OpenComic](https://github.com/ollm/OpenComic)|An open source comic and e-book reader that supports JPG, PNG, WEBP and other image formats, RAR, ZIP, 7Z and other compressed packages and PDF, EPUB documents, provides multi-reading mode, double-page reading, bookmark continuation and other functions, and is cross-platform compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems.

### Development Tools

Project|Description
---|---
[drawdb](https://github.com/drawdb-io/drawdb)|A powerful and easy-to-use online database design tool and SQL generator that supports creating charts, exporting SQL scripts and custom editors through simple operations. It is currently compatible with mainstream databases such as MySQL, PostgreSQL, SQLite, etc., and is completely open source and free, and can be used without registration.
[it-tools](https://github.com/CorentinTh/it-tools)|An online tool library specially created for developers, covering practical tools such as Token generator, data converter, timestamp converter, QR code generator, Git memo, etc. It supports direct online use and one-click deployment to the server through Docker.
[bruno](https://github.com/usebruno/bruno/)|An open source replacement API testing tool for Postman, it is powerful, lightweight and easy to use. It uses the plain text markup language Bru to save API information, supports Git version control and collaboration, and is limited to offline use to ensure data security and privacy.
[network_proxy_flutter](https://github.com/wanghongenpin/network_proxy_flutter)|A cross-platform open source packet capture tool developed based on Flutter. It supports interception, inspection and rewriting of HTTP(S) requests. It has mobile phone scan code connection, domain name filtering, request rewriting and shielding, JavaScript script processing and multi-condition search functions. It is compatible with Windows, Mac, Android, iOS and Linux systems.
[smallpond](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/smallpond)|A lightweight data processing framework based on DuckDB and 3FS. It is specially designed to handle PB-level data sets. It provides high-performance data processing capabilities without the need for long-term running services. It is easy to operate and supports Python 3.8 to 3.12 versions.
[3fs](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/3fs)|A high-performance distributed file system designed for AI training and inference workloads, leveraging modern SSD and RDMA networks to provide a shared storage layer, supporting aggregate read throughput up to 6.6 TiB/s, simplifying distributed application development.
[ai-wechat-bot](https://github.com/alex-oos/ai-wechat-bot)|A personal WeChat free open source framework that supports secondary development and multi-language access. There is no need to install computer WeChat or mobile phone cracking plug-ins. It provides message automation, customized replies, group management and business model access capabilities. It can send text, pictures and other message types.
[gitdiagram](https://github.com/ahmedkhaleel2004/gitdiagram)|An architecture visualization tool for GitHub projects. By replacing "hub" in the project link with "diagram", it can generate interactive diagrams in a few seconds to help users intuitively understand the project structure.
[git-who](https://github.com/sinclairtarget/git-who)|A command line tool for Git end users that can display the person responsible for the entire component or subsystem instead of a single file. You can view the main contributors and last edited information of the project.
[plane](https://github.com/makeplane/plane)|An open source project management tool for individuals or small teams. It can track issues, management cycles and product roadmaps. It provides burn-down charts to display progress in real time and custom views to filter tasks. It supports Docker and Kubernetes rapid deployment and is completely free.
[ghostty](https://github.com/ghostty-org/ghostty)|A high-looking open source terminal tool with fast startup, smooth scrolling and high I/O throughput, 2-4 times faster than traditional terminals. It is written in Zig language, supports GPU accelerated rendering, and provides a simple interface and an almost native experience.
[open-mcp-client](https://github.com/CopilotKit/open-mcp-client)|A client tool for interacting with the MCP server, which allows users to easily connect to the MCP server in any application. Just get the URL from Composio to quickly connect. It is based on web pages and can be used directly in the browser and is open source.
[ScrapeServ](https://github.com/goodreasonai/ScrapeServ)|A website screenshot and data capture tool on GitHub that can convert any website URL into a complete data file plus screenshots. It supports JavaScript execution in the browser environment. It can automatically scroll the page and take screenshots of different areas without complex configuration.
[certimate](https://github.com/usual2970/certimate)|An automated SSL certificate management tool that can automatically apply for, deploy and update SSL certificates. It supports pan-domain names and multiple domain names, covering 20+ domain name hosts and 50+ deployment targets. It can orchestrate flexible workflows to automate the entire certificate management process.
[youtube-api-server](https://github.com/zaidmukaddam/youtube-api-server)|A YouTube video information extraction tool specially prepared for developers, which can easily obtain video metadata, subtitles and timestamp transcripts. It is built based on FastAPI and is suitable for application development that processes YouTube content.
[posting](https://github.com/darrenburns/posting)|An HTTP client tool in a terminal environment, similar to Postman and Insomnia functions. It supports use through SSH and efficient keyboard operation processes. All requests are stored locally in simple YAML files, and has features such as fast navigation, environment variable settings, auto-completion, syntax highlighting, and Vim shortcut keys.
[mRemoteNG](https://github.com/mRemoteNG/mRemoteNG)|An open source multi-protocol remote connection management tool that provides a simple and powerful visual interface, integrating multiple protocols such as RDP, VNC, SSH, Telnet, PowerShell Remoting and HTTP/HTTPS, and can manage and view all connections in a unified interface.
[instant](https://github.com/instantdb/instant)|A modern Firebase alternative for front-end developers, providing real-time database capabilities. Automatically handles data acquisition, permission checking and offline caching through relational query description data shapes, and supports multi-person collaboration by default to improve development productivity.
[wiredoor](https://github.com/wiredoor/wiredoor)|An open source tool for intranet penetration based on WireGuard, which provides intuitive web interface management services and automatically configures SSL certificates. It exposes internal services to the public network through high-performance secure tunnels. The built-in NGINX reverse proxy reduces the complexity of use.
[pad.ws](https://github.com/pad-ws/pad.ws)|An open source tool for developers that integrates whiteboard and IDE development tools to support the entire process from conception to code implementation on the same interface, solving the problem of tool switching interrupting ideas and reducing efficiency during coding.
[git-sync](https://github.com/AkashRajpurohit/git-sync)|A Git warehouse backup and synchronization tool used to prevent GitHub account risks. It can automatically back up all Git warehouses locally to ensure that users can access the code at any time and prevent problems before they occur.
[tweakcn](https://github.com/jnsahaj/tweakcn)|A visual theme editing tool for front-end developers, which can visually customize Tailwind CSS and shadcn/ui components, and provides 300+ beautiful theme presets to quickly apply personalized styles.
[jisuxiang](https://github.com/star7th/jisuxiang)|An online development toolbox for programmers, integrating essential tools such as JSON processing, encoding and decoding, and network testing. It supports searching, collecting commonly used tools, and switching between dark and light themes. It adopts responsive design and is developed based on Node.js.
[DocFlow](https://github.com/xun082/DocFlow)|An open source solution for building an exclusive collaborative document platform that supports rich text editing, multi-person real-time collaboration, user rights management, plug-in extensions and other functions. It is built based on Tiptap and Next.js and integrates Yjs real-time collaboration technology.
[nexus-terminal](https://github.com/Heavrnl/nexus-terminal)|A browser-based server management tool that supports multiple SSH/RDP/VNC protocols, integrates file management and online editing functions, can manage connections through multiple tabs, and supports session suspension.
[spireflow](https://github.com/matt765/spireflow)|A complete management backend dashboard template that solves the problems of data chart display, user authentication and form system construction. It is built based on NextJS and TypeScript and has 14 built-in enterprise-level components such as data charts, complete form system and user authentication.
[cli](https://github.com/astracompiler/cli)|An open source compiler for front-end developers, which can directly compile JS/TS code into an independently runnable exe file. Based on esbuild, it achieves ultra-fast compilation speed. The generated file is only 70-80MB in weight. It supports the latest Node.js and ESM modules.
[SeleniumBase](https://github.com/seleniumbase/SeleniumBase)|A Python Web automation framework based on Selenium's deep encapsulation. The built-in intelligent waiting mechanism solves element loading problems, provides automatic driver management function without manual browser driver configuration, supports multiple writing methods such as BaseCase class inheritance and SB context manager, and UC Mode stealth mode to bypass detection.
[Cloudflare-SQL-to-API](https://github.com/123xiao/Cloudflare-SQL-to-API)|A tool for quickly building data APIs for front-end developers. REST API is directly generated by writing SQL without back-end code. It has web visual management interface, parameterized query and SQL injection protection functions, and achieves fast response based on Cloudflare Workers and D1 database.
[registry](https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/registry)|A centralized registry service for discovering and managing MCP servers. It provides RESTful API to implement addition, deletion, modification and query of server entries, paging query and health monitoring functions. It supports MongoDB and in-memory databases and can be deployed with one click through Docker.
[oh-my-git](https://github.com/git-learning-game/oh-my-git)|An open source tool for learning Git. It transforms abstract Git commands into an intuitive visual experience through carefully designed game levels, helping users quickly understand how Git works. It provides a gamified learning experience and level system design to master Git commands and workflow while playing.
[allinssl](https://github.com/allinssl/allinssl)|A one-stop SSL certificate management tool that automatically completes the entire process of certificate application, renewal, and deployment. It provides a visual certificate management interface that allows you to understand the status of all certificates at a glance.
[tproxy](https://github.com/kevwan/tproxy)|A lightweight network proxy open source tool for back-end development, it monitors and analyzes various TCP connection details in real time, supports multiple protocol identification, network status indicator viewing, connection pool analysis, packet delay forwarding and speed limit, and provides rapid deployment of Docker images.
[chsrc](https://github.com/RubyMetric/chsrc)|A universal source-changing tool and framework for all platforms. It automatically switches to available image sources through one line of commands. It has 60+ built-in source addresses to ensure availability. It supports multiple systems such as Linux and Windows as well as speed measurement and project-level source-changing functions.
[openresty-manager](https://github.com/Safe3/openresty-manager)|A visual management tool for OpenResty servers that provides reverse proxy configuration, free SSL certificate application, access control, HTTP flood prevention and other functions. It supports host and Docker deployment, and can manage multiple websites through the web interface without the need for professional skills.
[flutter_server_box](https://github.com/lollipopkit/flutter_server_box)|A full-platform server management toolbox for operation and maintenance engineers. It integrates core functions such as real-time monitoring of key indicators such as CPU and GPU, SSH terminal, SFTP file management, and Docker containers. It supports biometric authentication, push notifications, and desktop widgets, covering multiple platforms such as iOS, Android, Linux, and Windows.
[tracy](https://github.com/wolfpld/tracy)|An open source performance analysis tool for games or large applications. Real-time monitoring of application performance with nanosecond-level accuracy supports analysis of multi-dimensional data such as CPU, GPU, memory allocation, locks and context switching, and can automatically associate screenshots with captured frames to provide an intuitive visual performance view.
[gitsummarize](https://github.com/antarixxx/gitsummarize)|An open source document generation tool for GitHub code bases. Just enter the warehouse link to generate beautiful and easy-to-understand documents with one click. It provides clear project architecture diagrams, flow charts, catalog file outlines, and key business logic processing introductions.
[anycrawl](https://github.com/any4ai/anycrawl)|A high-performance web crawler and data scraping tool that integrates three engines: Cheerio, Playwright, and Puppeteer. It can quickly process static pages and handle complex JavaScript rendering content. It also supports batch crawling of Google search results.
[leetcode.nvim](https://github.com/kawre/leetcode.nvim)|A LeetCode auxiliary plug-in for the Neovim editor. You can view question descriptions, submit codes, and view personal statistics in the editor. Daily challenges and random questions are supported to facilitate users to answer questions efficiently.
[n8n-nodes-wechat-offiaccount](https://github.com/other-blowsnow/n8n-nodes-wechat-offiaccount)|A public account operation automation tool based on n8n, which encapsulates WeChat public account user management, material management and other functions into nodes, and builds automated processes through visual drag and drop. It is open source and free.
[auto-py-to-exe](https://github.com/brentvollebregt/auto-py-to-exe)|An open source tool for Python project packaging, which can convert Python projects into executable files, providing an intuitive graphical interface operation without complex command lines. You only need to select the script file, configuration options and click the conversion button to complete the packaging process.
[watercrawl](https://github.com/watercrawl/watercrawl)|A web crawler and data extraction tool based on Python + Scrapy, which can efficiently crawl web pages and extract relevant data. It supports custom crawling depth, speed and target content, and has multi-language content crawling and real-time progress monitoring capabilities.
[pyfuze](https://github.com/TanixLu/pyfuze)|A Python project packaging tool based on cosmopolitan and uv, converting Python projects into executable files with one click. It provides three modes: bundled, online and portable, supports cross-platform operation, customized configuration and GUI application packaging, and is compatible with macOS, Linux and Windows systems.
[planka](https://github.com/plankanban/planka)|A Kanban project management tool that serves as an open source alternative to Trello, Notion, and Asana. It supports a drag-and-drop Kanban interface for project, list, and card management, and provides an intuitive and easy-to-use interface, multi-person real-time collaboration, Markdown editing, and a flexible notification system.
[container](https://github.com/apple/container)|A tool for efficiently running Linux containers on macOS systems. It can create and manage lightweight Linux container virtual machines. It supports pulling standard container warehouse images. It is natively developed based on Swift and deeply optimized for Apple chips. It is fully compatible with OCI standard container images.
[torchvista](https://github.com/sachinhosmani/torchvista)|A PyTorch model visualization tool for deep learning developers that can analyze complex model structures with just one line of code. Provides an interactive graphical interface in notebooks such as Jupyter and Colab, supporting drag and zoom to view the model structure and click on nodes to view parameter details.
[json-schema-for-humans](https://github.com/coveooss/json-schema-for-humans)|A JSON Schema document generation tool for API development and configuration file writing. It can automatically convert JSON Schema into beautiful and easy-to-read static documents. It supports the generation of HTML and Markdown formats, provides a variety of template choices, is compatible with the JSON Schema Draft-07 standard, and intelligently handles complex reference relationships.
[hubproxy](https://github.com/sky22333/hubproxy)|A tool that improves the efficiency of resource acquisition. It can significantly speed up Docker image pulling, GitHub file and Hugging Face model downloading. It supports offline image package download, Docker image online search, and has custom black and white lists and intelligent current limiting functions.
[copyparty](https://github.com/9001/copyparty)|A file server tool running based on Python, supports breakpoint resume download and upload, is compatible with multiple web browsers, and can provide multiple protocol access at the same time including HTTP, WebDAV, FTP, TFTP, and SMB.
[fuck-u-code](https://github.com/Done-0/fuck-u-code)|A code quality assessment tool that analyzes the codes of mainstream programming languages ​​such as Go, JavaScript, Python, and Java through seven dimensions (complexity, function length, comment coverage, etc.), generates a "Shit Mountain Index" score of 0-100 points, and provides color terminal reports and flexible configuration options.
[raycast-linux](https://github.com/ByteAtATime/raycast-linux)|An efficiency tool for the Linux platform that implements the core functions of Raycast and supports application search, command execution, smart calculator, clipboard history and code snippet management. It is compatible with the Raycast extended ecosystem and can connect to AI models through OpenRouter.
[Gwitter](https://github.com/SimonAKing/Gwitter)|A lightweight personal Weibo platform built using GitHub Issues. [sniffnet](https://github.com/GyulyVGC/sniffnet)|A cross-platform network monitoring tool that helps users monitor all network traffic. It supports selecting network adapters and setting filter conditions. Real-time charts display traffic intensity and statistical information. It can identify 6000+ network services, protocols and malware, display the domain name, ASN and geographical location information of the remote host. It can also import and export PCAP files and customize notifications.
[linkedin_scraper](https://github.com/joeyism/linkedin_scraper)|A GitHub open source LinkedIn data crawling tool that supports comprehensive crawling of personal details, company introductions, job information and job search results, and has the ability to automatically handle login verification.
[lazysql](https://github.com/jorgerojas26/lazysql)|A database management tool for end users, which integrates many database operation functions into the terminal interface, supports cross-platform operation, Vim key binding, multi-database connection management and built-in SQL editor, and is compatible with mainstream databases such as MySQL and PostgreSQL.
[dify-schedule](https://github.com/leochen-g/dify-schedule)|An automation tool for Dify that solves the problem that Dify does not have a built-in scheduled automatic execution function. [dify-google-cloud-terraform](https://github.com/DeNA/dify-google-cloud-terraform)|An automated tool for deploying Dify on Google Cloud. [ui-experiments](https://github.com/origin-space/ui-experiments)|An open source UI layout template built on shadcn/ui. It provides 7 exquisite layouts such as Schema visualization, event calendar, encrypted wallet, and SaaS dashboard. It supports online preview effects and is suitable for solving the problem of lack of design inspiration. It is free for personal and commercial use.
[Termix](https://github.com/LukeGus/Termix)|A self-hosted server management platform that provides a Web interface to manage multiple servers in a unified manner. It supports SSH terminal split screen and tags, automatic tunnel reconnection, remote file editing, host information storage and classification, server status monitoring and user authentication functions. It can be deployed with one click through Docker Compose.
[xiaohongshu-mcp](https://github.com/xpzouying/xiaohongshu-mcp)|An MCP server for automated Xiaohongshu operations. It supports functions such as login status maintenance, image and text publishing, and content search. [mesh2motion-app](https://github.com/scottpetrovic/mesh2motion-app)|An open source tool for 3D animation production for game development. [streamdown](https://github.com/vercel/streamdown)|A framework specially designed for AI streaming Markdown rendering. It intelligently handles incomplete Markdown blocks to achieve smooth display. [doxx](https://github.com/bgreenwell/doxx)|A Word document viewer specially designed for terminals. Developed based on Rust, it starts quickly and takes up low memory. It provides practical functions such as search, navigation and multi-format export, and is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux systems.
[FossFLOW](https://github.com/stan-smith/FossFLOW)|A professional isometric chart production tool that supports 3D style technical architecture diagram drawing and custom icon import. [Xget](https://github.com/xixu-me/Xget)|A resource acceleration engine that solves network timeout and slow download problems. [open-lovable](https://github.com/mendableai/open-lovable)|A powerful website cloning tool that can regenerate an identical website based on React in just a few seconds by simply entering the target website link. It is open sourced by the Firecrawl team and quickly gained high attention on GitHub.
[tinyauth](https://github.com/steveiliop56/tinyauth)|A lightweight Docker application authentication middleware that can add a unified login verification interface to various web applications with one click. [tabiew](https://github.com/shshemi/tabiew)|A terminal table data processing tool that supports CSV, Parquet, JSON, Excel and other formats. It can quickly preview, filter, search, and draw. [snapdom](https://github.com/zumerlab/snapdom)|An efficient open source tool for converting DOM images into images. [refund-swatter-lite](https://github.com/argus-sight/refund-swatter-lite)|An open source solution to help iOS developers deal with malicious refunds. [fullstack-next-cloudflare](https://github.com/ifindev/fullstack-next-cloudflare)|A full-stack project template based on Cloudflare and Next.js, integrating complete functions such as database, storage, AI inference and front-end framework. [nginx-ignition](https://github.com/lucasdillmann/nginx-ignition)|An open source tool for intuitive and visual management of Nginx configuration. [flat](https://github.com/netless-io/flat)|An open source online classroom solution for teachers and training institutions. It supports multiple platforms including Web, Windows and macOS, and can be run locally by installing dependencies through pnpm.
[rustnet](https://github.com/domcyrus/rustnet)|An open source monitoring tool designed for real-time network connection analysis. [oq](https://github.com/plutov/oq)|A terminal tool for browsing OpenAPI documents. You can view the latest documents directly on the terminal. [memory_graph](https://github.com/bterwijn/memory_graph)|An open source tool for Python data structure visualization. [broot](https://github.com/Canop/broot)|A terminal directory browsing and management tool that supports intelligent filtering and tree structure display of large project directories. It provides fuzzy search, file preview, multi-panel operation, Git status integration and custom command binding functions. It is compatible with Windows, macOS and Linux platforms.
[git-filter-repo](https://github.com/newren/git-filter-repo)|A tool for rewriting Git warehouse history, which can completely clean up sensitive information in submissions. [term.everything](https://github.com/mmulet/term.everything)|An open source tool for Linux terminal environment, which can run browsers, games, videos and other GUI applications directly on the terminal. It is compatible with terminals such as kitty and iTerm2 and achieves full-resolution rendering. It supports SSH remote operation without X11 forwarding, providing a smooth usage experience.
[surf](https://github.com/enetx/surf)|A feature-rich open source HTTP client for web crawlers to handle complex request scenarios. It has powerful browser fingerprint camouflage capabilities, supports JA3/JA4 fingerprint customization and HTTP/3 QUIC protocol, and can accurately simulate the characteristics of mainstream browsers while maintaining full compatibility with the standard library.
[sshm](https://github.com/Gu1llaum-3/sshm)|A cross-platform SSH connection management tool. It provides a high-looking terminal interface and intuitive interaction, supports quick connection to configured hosts, port forwarding settings, tag classification management and intelligent search and filtering functions, and is compatible with existing ~/.ssh/config files.
[winboat](https://github.com/TibixDev/winboat)|An open source tool for running Windows applications on Linux systems. [structured-prompt-builder](https://github.com/Siddhesh2377/structured-prompt-builder)|An open source tool for building high-quality prompt words. [codeforge](https://github.com/devlive-community/codeforge)|A lightweight desktop code executor that supports one-click execution of 20+ mainstream programming languages, providing a simple editor and real-time result display. [oryx](https://github.com/pythops/oryx)|An open source terminal network tool for operation and maintenance personnel and network engineers. [rendergit](https://github.com/karpathy/rendergit)|A tool for GitHub project code viewing. It can convert the project into a single-page static web page with one click and display all files. [diagram-as-code](https://github.com/awslabs/diagram-as-code)|An open source tool for generating AWS architecture diagrams. [docmd](https://github.com/mgks/docmd)|An open source tool that converts Markdown files into static document websites. It can quickly generate beautiful and lightweight websites. [shell360](https://github.com/nashaofu/shell360)|An open source cross-platform SSH client that provides complete SSH connection functions (supports password, key, and certificate authentication) and powerful SFTP file transfer. [x-ui-pro](https://github.com/GFW4Fun/x-ui-pro)|A visual tool for building private proxy services. [getk](https://github.com/dennisyang1986/getk)|An open source tool for quantitative trading research and stock data analysis. [GodSVG](https://github.com/MewPurPur/GodSVG)|A structured SVG vector editor that combines an intuitive visual interface with code editing functions to instantly generate clean and readable underlying code when operating graphic elements without adding any metadata. [OSX-PROXMOX](https://github.com/luchina-gabriel/OSX-PROXMOX)|An open source tool for users who have never experienced macOS system. [pipet](https://github.com/bjesus/pipet)|An open source crawler tool developed based on Go language. It supports curl and Playwright crawling methods, HTML parsing and other modes, and has timing monitoring, automatic page turning, JSON output and Unix pipeline integration functions.
[browserless](https://github.com/browserless/browserless)|An open source headless browser deployment tool for server automation tasks. It is compatible with multiple browsers and can be started with one click without complex configuration.
[homepage](https://github.com/gethomepage/homepage)|A self-hosted service management tool that can centrally display multiple applications and monitor service status and system resources. [tiny-rdm](https://github.com/tiny-craft/tiny-rdm)|A lightweight Redis management desktop application specially built for developers. [gpu-hot](https://github.com/psalias2006/gpu-hot)|An open source tool for managing multiple GPU servers. It can visually and uniformly monitor GPU resources in the browser. It is deployed through Docker commands and the WebSocket real-time update response speed is less than 1 second.
[yaak](https://github.com/mountain-loop/yaak)|An API testing desktop application specially built for developers who focus on privacy and performance. [Ducky](https://github.com/thecmdguy/Ducky)|An open source desktop application for network engineers, integrating more than a dozen commonly used tools such as SSH clients and network scanners, and providing functions such as multi-protocol terminal connection, automatic network topology discovery and visualization, subnet calculation and security toolkit. [p2p-live-share](https://github.com/kermanx/p2p-live-share)|A remote collaboration development plug-in for VSCode. [systemctl-tui](https://github.com/rgwood/systemctl-tui)|A terminal interface tool for managing Linux system services. [Gitnuro](https://github.com/JetpackDuba/Gitnuro)|A free open source Git client for beginners, covering daily Git operations from basic submission and push to advanced rebase and interactive operations. [pyscn](https://github.com/ludo-technologies/pyscn)|A quality analysis tool for AI-generated Python code. [desktop](https://github.com/atuinsh/desktop)|An open source desktop application that integrates document and terminal operations. [cutter](https://github.com/rizinorg/cutter)|An open source reverse engineering platform for binary file analysis and malware reverse engineering. [codeimage](https://github.com/riccardoperra/codeimage)|A code screenshot tool specially built for developers, it supports syntax highlighting in multiple programming languages ​​and provides a wealth of themes, backgrounds and customization options.
[BotBrowser](https://github.com/botswin/BotBrowser)|An open source browser tool based on the Chromium kernel. [brook](https://github.com/g-brook/brook)|A cross-platform network tunnel and proxy tool for developers, which can uniformly manage intranet penetration tunnels. [git-worktree-runner](https://github.com/coderabbitai/git-worktree-runner)|A command line tool that simplifies the use of Git Worktree. It can quickly create a workspace through simple commands, supports development tool integration, automatically copies configuration files and environment variables, and provides Shell Tab completion function to improve parallel development efficiency.
[SourceCodeSyntaxHighlight](https://github.com/sbarex/SourceCodeSyntaxHighlight)|A Quick Look extension tool for macOS system, used for code file preview. It supports highlighting of more than 200 programming languages, can customize theme color and font style, integrates Git version control difference highlighting, and provides command line batch conversion function.
[shot-scraper](https://github.com/simonw/shot-scraper)|An open source screenshot tool for technical document maintenance. It is easy to install and suitable for developers and content creators.
[builder](https://github.com/frappe/builder)|An open source low-code website building tool for personal work display and company official website production. [tuios](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/tuios)|A terminal multi-window management tool that supports the creation of 9 independent workspaces and automatic tile layout. It has built-in SSH server mode to realize remote terminal multiplexing. It provides Vim-style copy mode to support scrolling review and search of thousands of lines of logs. [postgresus](https://github.com/RostislavDugin/postgresus)|An automated backup tool for PostgreSQL databases that supports the execution of backup plans according to custom cycles and times. It is deployed based on Docker and is compatible with PostgreSQL versions 13-18.
[docker-jdownloader-2](https://github.com/jlesage/docker-jdownloader-2)|A Docker deployment tool for JDownloader 2. You can use the complete download function through browser access. It supports VNC remote control, mobile phone management download tasks and built-in Web file manager.
[PyFRC2G](https://github.com/olivierb46/PyFRC2G)|An open source tool for firewall rule visualization, which can automatically convert pfSense and OPNSense rules into flow charts. [likec4](https://github.com/likec4/likec4)|An open source project for drawing system architecture diagrams. It automatically generates real-time updated architecture diagrams through code description architecture, and supports custom element types, relationship symbols and any level of nested structure.
[pipet](https://github.com/0xSojalSec/pipet)|A command line crawler tool that automatically crawls web page content through the data structure defined in the configuration file. [fresh](https://github.com/sinelaw/fresh)|A visual editor for terminal environments that supports mouse interaction, universal shortcut keys, and efficient processing of large files. [svg-path-editor](https://github.com/Yqnn/svg-path-editor)|An SVG path visual editing tool for design and front-end development. [serie](https://github.com/lusingander/serie)|A terminal Git commit history visualization tool that renders high-quality commit graphs in the form of pictures. [lsv](https://github.com/SecretDeveloper/lsv)|A three-column terminal file browser tool that provides upper-level directory, current directory display and real-time preview of files. [pinme](https://github.com/glitternetwork/pinme)|A front-end application deployment tool that can launch applications in seconds with just one line of commands. [hedgedoc](https://github.com/hedgedoc/hedgedoc)|A real-time collaborative note-taking tool for small team collaboration and knowledge management. [mind-elixir-core](https://github.com/ssshooter/mind-elixir-core)|A lightweight and high-performance open source JavaScript mind mapping tool. [gpui-component](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-component)|A cross-platform desktop UI component library based on GPUI, providing 40+ native style components, integrating macOS and Windows designs, and supporting cross-platform development needs.

[fusion-pixel-font](https://github.com/TakWolf/fusion-pixel-font)|A pan-Chinese, Japanese and Korean pixel font that supports three pixel sizes of 8px, 10px, and 12px. It adopts a bold sans-serif style and provides specific glyph versions for different languages.
[cap](https://github.com/tiagorangel1/cap)|A lightweight website verification code open source library for front-end developers, which can be used as an alternative to CAPTCHA. It supports multiple display methods such as visible, invisible, and floating. [plutofilter](https://github.com/sammycage/plutofilter)|A high-performance single-header file image filter library, compatible with SVG and CSS filter semantics, supports Gaussian blur, color transformation, blending mode and other effects and chain calls, achieving zero memory allocation.
[tinylisp](https://github.com/Robert-van-Engelen/tinylisp)|A Lisp interpreter implemented in 99 lines of C code, including 21 built-in functions, garbage collection mechanism and REPL interactive environment, and can run on a 1980s handheld computer.
[Sensitive-lexicon](https://github.com/Konsheng/Sensitive-lexicon)|A Chinese sensitive thesaurus for content review, which collects tens of thousands of high-quality sensitive words covering common risk scenarios. It can be used in review systems and text preprocessing by combining algorithms such as DFA and Trie.
[googlesans-code](https://github.com/googlefonts/googlesans-code)|A fixed-width font specially designed for programmers, which effectively solves the problem of visual fatigue caused by long-term programming. It is optimized for programming language syntax to ensure that small-size characters are clear and legible. It supports variable font weights of 300-800.
[sj.h](https://github.com/rxi/sj.h)|An ultra-lightweight JSON parsing library that provides error location accurate to rows and columns, can completely control the data parsing process, supports flexible data processing, adopts a zero memory allocation design, only 150 lines of C99 code, and can be used for free and unlimited use using the Unlicense protocol.
[jdenticon](https://github.com/dmester/jdenticon)|A lightweight JavaScript library for generating user avatars. It generates unique geometric pattern avatars based on any string. It supports two rendering methods: Canvas and SVG. It is small in size after compression and provides rich custom configuration options.
[mediabunny](https://github.com/Vanilagy/mediabunny)|An audio and video processing JavaScript library specially built for the Web. It allows reading, writing and converting media files in various formats directly in the browser. It provides editing functions such as transcoding and resizing. It is written in pure TypeScript and has 25+ built-in codecs. It supports microsecond-level operations and hardware acceleration, efficiently processes files of any size through memory and provides ultimate tree-shaking optimization.
[Scrapling](https://github.com/D4Vinci/Scrapling)|An adaptive crawler open source library for developers that can automatically reposition target elements after website structure changes. It has built-in multiple anti-crawler bypass technologies such as TLS fingerprint camouflage and Cloudflare verification processing. It supports rich selectors and high-performance parsing engines such as CSS and XPath.
[lurk](https://github.com/lurk-lab/lurk)|An open source programming language for zero-knowledge proof application development, allowing developers to focus on writing business logic just like writing ordinary programs. [MathFlow](https://github.com/cybergeek1943/MathFlow)|An open source library for mathematical calculations that provides a unified interface to seamlessly connect symbolic operations and numerical calculations. It is built on SymPy and has automatic numerical conversion, LaTeX formula and natural mathematical symbol analysis functions, and enhanced log printing output to improve debugging efficiency.
[Crawler-Detect](https://github.com/JayBizzle/Crawler-Detect)|An open source PHP library for website development and data analysis. It identifies crawler access records by analyzing User Agent and HTTP Header information. It supports the detection of thousands of crawlers, outputs specific names, integrates mainstream frameworks, and provides multi-programming language versions.
[translators](https://github.com/UlionTse/translators)|A Python open source library that integrates 37 mainstream translation platforms, providing a unified API interface to realize translation functions in more than 450 languages. It can be used for free without applying for each platform key. It supports text and HTML content translation, comparison of translation quality on different platforms, and direct invocation of command line tools.
[Monocraft](https://github.com/IdreesInc/Monocraft)|A programming font that integrates the Minecraft UI pixel style into a monowidth design. It contains 1500+ carefully adjusted characters to adapt to the monowidth environment. It supports the programming ligature function to make arrow symbols and comparison operators more intuitive and easy to read.
[AppUpdater](https://github.com/s1ntoneli/AppUpdater)|A streamlined open source library that provides automatic update capabilities for macOS applications. It uses GitHub Releases to achieve silent updates. It is compatible with SwiftUI and supports multi-language update log display and proxy configuration. After reconstruction, it removes PromiseKit dependencies and uses async/await syntax instead, and has code signature verification functions.
[shapash](https://github.com/MAIF/shapash)|A Python library for machine learning model interpretation. It can translate complex algorithm logic into a visual language, generate an interactive Web dashboard to display feature influence weights, and support global to local exploration. It is equipped with business tags and is compatible with mainstream model frameworks. It can generate HTML audit reports and provide lightweight predictors for production environment deployment.
[python-markdownify](https://github.com/matthewwithanm/python-markdownify)|A Python utility tool for HTML to Markdown conversion, which accurately restores complex HTML strings or files into clearly structured Markdown text. It supports highly customized configurations, can filter specific tags, adjust title styles, process tables and images, and allows inherited classes to rewrite conversion rules to meet special business needs.
[article-extractor](https://github.com/extractus/article-extractor)|An open source library for extracting the core content of web pages. [leantime](https://github.com/Leantime/leantime)|An open source project management tool that combines ease of use and professionalism. It supports multi-view task management such as Kanban boards, Gantt charts, tables, etc. It can be operated as simply as Feishu Kanban, but also has Zen Tao-level functional depth.
[ZeroTrace-Stealer-13-2026](https://github.com/luis22d/ZeroTrace-Stealer-13-2026)|An open source monitoring tool for Windows systems that can steal Chrome browser passwords, cookies and other information from the client and send it to the server. It has real-time monitoring, data collection and analysis functions, and provides an intuitive interface, custom port configuration and client builder.
[obsidian-velocity](https://github.com/Gonzalo-D-Sales/obsidian-velocity)|A theme that redefines the Obsidian interface imagination. It has an intelligent adaptive interface, rich CSS class support, carefully adjusted typography details and powerful Callout style. The author has spent nearly a year carefully polishing it in accordance with Apple's design principles.
[OpenEmu](https://github.com/OpenEmu/OpenEmu)|A native application that integrates multiple classic game console emulators, supports the simulation of more than 30 classic game consoles, and can play retro games such as Super Mario and Contra, integrating many emulator functions into one.
[Nook](https://github.com/nook-browser/Nook)|A simple open source browser with a sidebar design that allows you to view it clearly even if you have many tabs open. The size is only 8.42 MB, it runs fast and uses low memory. The minimalist interface design helps you focus on content browsing.
